12. tāmē tōṉṟum uṇarccikaḷ
itukāṟum iruvar uṇarvōrum uṇarttu vōrumāy arukil iruntun tolivil iruntum oruvar eṇṇaṅkaḷi maṟṟavar uṇarum muṟimikaḷi viḷakkip pōntām. iṉi, iṅṅaṉam paḻakātavar cilar tamakkuḷḷun tolivil nikaḻum nikaḻccikaḷ tāmākavē uḷḷattil eḻum vakikaḷiyuñ ciṟitu iṅkeṭuttup pēcuvām. oruvariṭattu oruvar mikunta aṉpu vittirukkum kātalaṉuṅ kātaliyum oruvari oruvar akaṉṟu tolivilirukkuṅkāl oruvareṇṇaṅkaḷiyum avarkku nērvaṉavaṟṟiyum maṟṟavar terintukoḷḷa muyalā tirukkiyilum avi avaruḷḷattil tāmākavē vantu tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. itaṟku uṇmiyāy nikaḻnta cila nikaḻccikaḷi iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām.
taṉ vāyiṉmēl kaṭumiyāṉa ōr aṭipaṭṭatāka uṇarntu ōr ammi orunāḷ tiṭukkeṉat tūkkattiṉiṉṟum eḻuntu, taṉ kīḻ utaṭu aṟuppuṇṭu irattam oḻukuvatākavē taṉakkut teḷivākappaṭṭataṉāl, taṉatu kikkuṭṭiyi eṭuttuc curuṭṭi avviṭattil vittukkoṇṭē taṉ paṭukkimēṟ ciṟitunēram uṭkārntu koṇṭiruntāḷ. ciṟitunērañ ceṉṟapiṉ avaḷ attuṇiyi vāyiṉiṉṟum akaṟṟip pārkka atil irattakkaṟi ciṟitum illātirukkak kaṇṭu peritum viyappuṟṟut taṉakku aṅṅaṉam aṭipaṭṭirukka muṭiyāteṉṟum, aṭipaṭṭatākak kaṇṭatu kaṉavēyaṉṟip piṟitu aṉṟeṉṟum uṇarntāḷ. uṭaṉē avaḷ taṉ kikkaṭikāratti nōkka appōtu viṭiyaṟkāliyil ēḻumaṇiyā yirukkak kaṇṭāḷ. appōtu taṉ kaṇavaṉ aṅku illāmi terintu, ak kālippoḻutu  mikavum iṉitāyiruntamiyāl, avaṉ aṭuttuḷḷa ēriyil viḷiyāṭṭup paṭaku viṭap pōyirukka vēṇṭu
meṉṟu muṭivukaṭṭiṉāḷ. ataṉpiṟ ciṟitunēran tūṅki
yeḻuntu kāli oṉpatari maṇikkuc ciṟṟuṇṭi koḷḷum poruṭṭu avaḷ oḻuṅkuceykiyil avaḷ kaṇavaṉ ciṟitunēraṅ kaḻittu vīṭṭukku vantāṉ. uṇaveṭukkum kāl vaḻakkampōl avaṉ avaḷ pakkattil amarāmaṟciṟitu eṭṭa iruntukoṇṭu, iṭikkiṭiyē taṉatu kikkuṭṭiyi eṭuttuc curuṭṭi avaḷukkut teriyāmal maṟivāy avaṉ taṉvāyil oṟṟikkoḷvatik kuṟippāyp pārttāḷ. pārttatum pataṟip perumāṉē, ēṉ appaṭic ceykiṟīrkaḷ? eṉṟu kavaliyoṭu kēṭṭu, nīṅkaḷ kāyappaṭuttik koṇṭīrkaḷ eṉpati nāṉ aṟivēṉ, āṉāl atippaṟṟip piṟaku collukiṟēṉ, eṉa moḻintāḷ. ataṟku avaṉ, nallatu, nāṉ paṭakiṟ pāyvirittuc celkiyil tiṭīreṉa oru kaṭuṅkāṟṟu vantu cukkāṉ tiruppuñ cakkarattic cuḻaṟṟiviṭavē aḵtu eṉvāyiṉ kīḻ utaṭṭil naṉṟāy aṭittuviṭṭatu. ataṉāl aṭipaṭṭa kāyattiṉiṉṟum irattam ōyāmal oḻukukiṟatu, eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ. ataṉpiṉ avaḷ, atu nērntapōtu maṇi evvaḷavu irukkalām? uṅkaḷukkut teriyumā? eṉṟu kēṭka, appōtu ēṟakkuṟiya ēḻumaṇi yirukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu viṭi kūṟiṉāḷ. ataṉpiṉ avaḷ taṉakku uṇṭāṉa tōṟṟatti virittuccolla avaṉuṅkūṭa iruntavarkaḷum mika viyappuṟṟaṉar.
oru nāḷ oru maruttuvar oru vīṭṭiṉ mettimēl nōyāḷi oruvarip pārttuviṭṭuk kīḻ iṟaṅkukiyil, nāṉku āṇṭuḷḷa avartañ ciṟumakaṉ tamatu vīṭṭuk kaṟpaṭik kaṭṭiṉ kīḻviḻuntu naṉṟāyk kāyappaṭṭatāka avarkkuc caṭutiyil oru niṉivu uṇṭāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam mutaliṟṟōṉṟiya anniṉivukkup piṉ,  iṭiyil iṭappaṭṭirunta oru tirimaṟippu appuṟam iḻukkap paṭṭāṟpōṟ kāṇappaṭa, ak kuḻanti paṭikkaṭṭiṉ kīḻē viḻuntu kiṭakkavum ataṉ mōvāyil irattam oḻukavum nērē kāṇpārāyiṉar. intat tōṟṟamāṉatu tiṭīreṉa maṟintupōyiṟṟu; āṉāl, ataṉip paṟṟiya niṉivu maṭṭum avarkkiruntatu. at tōṟṟam uṇṭāṉa nēraṅ kāli pattari maṇiyātalāl ataṉi maṭṭum avar kuṟittuk koṇṭār. piṟaku, tām pārkka vēṇṭiya maṟṟi nōyāḷikaḷiyum pārttuviṭṭu avar tamatu vīṭṭukkuppōka aṅkē avartaṅ kuṭumpattār avar kuḻantikku nērntatiyum maṇi nērattiyum avar kaṇṭapaṭiyē kūṟak kēṭṭu mika viyappuṟṟār.
oru peṇpiḷḷiyik kaṭumiyāka aṭittamiyāṟ ciṟik kōṭṭattil aṭikkappaṭṭup piṉṉar maṉṉittu viṭutali ceyyappaṭṭa oruvarkku oru maṉiviyum aintu āṇṭuḷḷa oru piyaṉum iruntaṉar. avar ciṟik
kōṭṭattil iruntapōtu orunāḷ avaratu maṉiviyiṭa
miruntu oru ceyti ak kōṭṭattalivarkku vantatu. aṟiviṉmikka avvāṇpiḷḷi iṟantu pōyiṟ ṟeṉavum ataṉit taṉ kaṇavaṟkut terivittal vēṇṭumeṉavum at talivar atiṟ kēṭṭukkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. ataṉpaṭiyē talivar, avarit tām irunta aṟikku varuvittu avarip paṟṟic cila viṉāviyapiṉ tām oru tuyaramāṉa ceyti terivikkap pōvatākac coṉṉār. ataṟku avar, aiyā, tāṅkaḷ collappōvatu iṉṉateṉpati aṟivēṉ: eṉ piyaṉ iṟantupōyiṉāṉ eṉṟār. uṭaṉē talivar, ataṉi nīr eppaṭi aṟintīr? eṉṟu kēṭṭār. ceṉṟa ñāyiṟṟukkiḻami tāṅkaḷun taṅkaḷ ciṟu kuḻantiyuṅ kōyilukku vantiruntapōtu antak kuḻanti tūṅkiviṭṭatu; ataṉit tāṅkaḷ kiyil eṭuttukkoṇṭu varuvati yāṉ pārttavuṭaṉē, eṉ piyaṉ nīril amiḻnti iṟantu pōṉatākac caṭutiyil eṉakkōr eṇṇam uṇṭāyiṟṟu, an niṉivi eṉ maṉattiṉiṉṟum akaṟṟa yāṉ evvaḷavō muyaṉṟum eṉṉāl iyalavilli! eṉ kaṇkaḷil nīr oḻukiṟṟu; nīṅkaḷ eṉṉi aḻittapōtu eṉneñcaṅ kalaṅkiyatu, nāṉ eṇṇiyapaṭitāṉ irukkap pōkiṟatu eṉṟu uṇarntēṉ, eṉa avar maṟumoḻi pukaṉṟār. ivar tam kuḻantimēl mikunta aṉpu vittiruntamiyāl ap piḷḷikku nērntatu ivarkkuṭaṉē tōṉṟalāyiṟṟu.
mēlnāṭṭil nūl ārāycciyiṟ ṟērnta ōr aṟiñar orunāḷ ōr iḷampeṇṇit tāmirukkuṅ kalik kaḻakattil vittu aṟituyiliṟ pōkac ceytār. oru kaṭita vuṟikkuḷ varintu vikkappaṭṭa oru varivaṭiviṉi iṉṉateṉṟu aṟiyumporuṭṭu avvammi avvaṟituyil niliyilēyē muyaṉṟu koṇṭirukkiyil, av vaṟiñar tam naṇpar oruvar peyaric colli avarkku ippōtu yātu nērntatu? eṉṟu kēṭṭār. an naṇpari anta ammi ik kalikkaḻakattil itaṟku muṉ iraṇṭu mūṉṟumuṟi pārttirukkiṟāḷ. avar tam uṭampiṟ cuṭṭukkoṇṭār, eṉṟu antap peṇ viṭi kūṟiṉāḷ. ataṟku avar, ō! avar evviṭattic cuṭṭukkoṇṭār? eṉṟu tirumpa viṉaviṉār. iṭatu kiyiṉmēl; cuṭṭatu neruppu aṉṟu; aḵtu ētō oṉṟu; ataṉ peyar eṉakkut teriyavilli; avar ataṉi ūṟṟiṉapōtu eṉ karuttāy irukkavilli? eṉṟu anta iḷamātu kūṟiṉāḷ. avar ūṟṟiṉa antap poruḷ enta niṟamāṉatu? atu civappu niṟamaṉṟu, paḻuppu niṟam uṭiyatu; avar mikutiyāyc cuṭṭukkoṇṭār - tōl avviṭattil atittirukkiṉṟatu eṉṟāḷ. ivaḷ kaṇṭu kūṟiyatu attaṉiyum vaḻuvātiruntatu. aṉṟip piṟpakal nāṉku maṇikku annaṇpar oru puṭṭiliṟ ciṟitu muṭinīri1 ūṟṟal vēṇṭiṉār. ataṉi ūṟṟukiṟapōtu avar nirampak karuttāyc ceyyāmiyāl, atu peykuḻalil nirampi vaḻintu ataṉip piṭittirunta avaratu iṭatu kimmēl ōṭi atupaṭṭa iṭamellāñ cuṭṭuviṭṭatu. avar uṭaṉē akkiyit taṇnīril tōyttārāyiṉum, am muṭinīr paṭṭa iṭamellāṅ koppuḷittup pōyiṟṟu.
iṉṉum, ōr ammi taṉakkut tōṉṟiya tōṟṟattip piṉvarumāṟu kuṟikkiṉṟāḷ; yāṉ kuḷir kāyccaliṟ kiṭantu nalam aṭintu varukiyil, yāṉ eṉatu aṟiyil taṉiyē iruntu cāḷarattiṉ veḷiyē pārttukkoṇṭiruntēṉ. eṉ eṇṇamāṉatu oṉṟiluṅ karuttāy nillāmaṟ palavaṟṟiṟ ceṉṟu koṇṭiruntatu; ciṟitu nērattiṟkup piṉ eṉ uḷḷamāṉatu ētoru niṉivumiṉṟi veṟumiyākak kāṇappaṭṭatu; eṉ kaṇkaḷ nilipeṟṟu niṟpavāyiṉa; iṭiveḷiyāṉatu veḷḷiya niṟam aṭivatupōṟ kāṇappaṭṭatu. eṉṉic cuṟṟilum uḷḷa poruḷkaḷi yāṉ pārkkak kūṭiyatā yiruntālum, etaṉiyum pārppatu mika varuttamākavē iruntatu. evarō oruvar - avar iṉṉā reṉpatum iṉṉa iṭattiṉa reṉpatum yāṉ aṟiyēṉ, tuṉpappaṭuvatāka eṉ maṉattiṟ paṭṭamiyāl yāṉ avar poruṭṭut tuṉpappaṭuvatāka uṇarntēṉ. ciṟitu nērattiṟkup piṉ avar yār eṉpatit terintēṉ; avari eppaṭit terintēṉ eṉpati aṟintilēṉ. āḷ iṉṉār eṉpati yāṉ terinta piṟaku, oru viṭutiyiṟ caturamāṉa ōr aṟiyiṉiyum, yāṉ iṉṉāreṉṟu terinta avvāṇmakaṉ mikavun tuṉpuṟṟa uṭampōṭum uḷḷattōṭum atil oru paṭukkimēl mukaṅ kuppuṟappaṭuttiruppatiyum eṉṉuḷḷē teḷivāyk kaṇṭēṉ. avar mikka tuyarattāl tēmpit tēmpi aḻutaliyuñ, ceviyāṟ kēṭṭilēṉāyiṉum, uḷḷattāl uṇarntēṉ. at tuyarattiṉ taṉmiyum eṉakkup pulaṉāyiṟṟu. āṉāl, puṟattēyuḷḷa ataṉ ētu eṉakkup pulappaṭṭilatu. ittōṟṟattiṟkup piṉ eṉakku mikunta kaḷippu uṇṭāyiṟṟu; atu nāṟpatu nimiṭaṅkaḷ variyil mikutiyāy iruntatu; ataṉpiṉ atu mella mellak kuṟintu pōyiṟṟu.
ivvāṟu antap peṇpiḷḷi tāṉkaṇṭa tōṟṟap
paṭi tuṉpuṟṟa āṇmakaṉukku ataṉi oru kaṭitattiṟkuṟittu viṭukka, avar piṉvarumāṟu eḻutukiṟār.
cila āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ yāṉ ōr aluvaliṉ poruṭṭu rōṭuttīviṉkaṇ uḷḷa oru nakarattil irukkiyiṟ, pāṭaṉ nakarattiṉkaṇ uḷḷa eṉ vīṭṭiliruntu caṭutiyil vanta ceytiyāṉatu mikavun tuṉpattit taruvatāy eṉṉi aṟakkalakkiviṭṭamiyāl yāṉ iṟaṅkiyirunta viṭutiyil eṉakkeṉa viṭappaṭṭa nāṟcaturamāṉa periya aṟikkuṭ ceṉṟu eṉatu paṭukkimēl neṭunēram peruntuyarattoṭu mukaṅkaviḻntu kiṭantēṉ. iṅṅaṉam neṭunērañ ceṉṟa piṟaku at tuṉpattiṉ kaṭumi taṇintamiyāl avvaṟiyiviṭṭu vantēṉ. aṭuttanāḷ yāṉ pāṭaṉ nakarattiṟkuc ceṉṟēṉ. ataṟku aṭuttanāḷ eṉatu tuyarattiṉ tōṟṟattikkaṇṭa peṇmaṇiyāriṉ kaṭitam eṉakku vantatu. avvammi mēli niyūyārkkil uḷḷa oru nakarattiṉiṉṟum nāṭkuṟittu akkaṭitatti eṉakku aṉuppiṉār. veḷḷikkiḻami iraṇṭu maṇikku eṉakku yātu nērntatu eṉpatik kālantāḻātu tamakkut terivikkumpaṭi avvammiyār keñcik kēṭṭār. atēnāḷil atē maṇi nērattiṟṟāṉ nāṉ muṉṉē kūṟiyapaṭi tūyarappaṭṭuk kiṭantēṉ. inta mātarār eṉakku aṟimukamāṉa naṇparēyāyiṉum, ivar aṉuppiya ik kuṟippukku muṉ pala tiṅkaḷāka ivariṭamiruntu ētoru ceytiyumē eṉakku varavilli; neṭuṅkālamāka inta ammiyip paṟṟi yāṉ niṉikkavumilli. iṉṉum, eṉṉi avvaḷavu tuṉpuṟuttiya acceytikkum anta ammikkum ētōr icivumilli. avvammikaṇṭatu uṇmiyēyeṉa uṭaṉē yāṉ avarkku eḻuti aṟivittēṉ. in nikaḻcaciyiṉāl, oruvari yoruvar niṉiyāmal vēṟu vēṟiṭaṅkaḷil irunta viṭattum oruvarkku uṇṭāṉa tuṉpam maṟṟavarkkut tāṉē pulaṉākat tōṉṟiṉami naṉku aṟiyappaṭum.
iṉip, palarukkut tītāṉa oru nikaḻcci eṅkāyiṉum naṭantāl atu toliviluḷḷa cilarkkut tāṉākavē vantu tōṉṟutalum uṇṭu. pert eṉṉum ūriṉ kaṇ orunāḷ ōr ammi taṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāḷukkāka oru kaṭitam eḻutik koṇṭirukkiyil oru koṭumpuyaṟ kāṟṟu avaḷatu vīṭṭic cuṟṟi aṭittatu. appōtu tiṭīreṉṟu avaḷuḷḷattil oru peruntikil uṇṭāyiṟṟu. avaḷ tāṉ eḻutuṅ kaṭitattil taṉ niṉivi niṟuttak kūṭātavaḷāṉāḷ. cāviyun tītāṉa nikaḻcciyiyum paṟṟiya eṇṇaṅkaḷ avaḷ uḷḷattil nili peṟṟut tōṉṟiṉa. makkaḷ palar aḻintu pōṉatip paṟṟiya tikilāṉa ōr uṇarcciyiyaṉṟi, eṅkē eppaṭi eppoḻutu aḻintārkaḷeṉṟu tiṭṭamāka avaḷukkuvēṟētun tōṉṟavilli. uṭaṉē avaḷ mēlmāḷikikkup paṟantu ceṉṟu taṉ tāyiṭam aṭintāḷ. mtŸ m‹id, ‘FHªjhŒ, <bj‹d kl¤jd«! eṉṟu kaṭintāḷ. āṉāl, ap peṇmaṇi illi, peruntiraḷāṉa māntarkaḷ eṅkō maṭikiṉṟami tiṇṇam. eṉṟāḷ. avaḷukkut tōṉṟiyati maṟunāḷ vanta ceyti uṟutippaṭuttiyatu; avaḷukkut tikilāṉa uṇarvu tōṉṟiya atēnērattil tē eṉṉum yāṟṟiṉ pālam iṭintuviḻap peruntiraḷāṉa makkaḷ kīḻviḻuntu māyntārkaḷ. it tōṟṟattik kaṇṭa peṇṇiṉ aṉṉikku uyirnili ārāyccikkaḻakattār itip paṟṟi eḻutikkēṭṭapōtu avaḷ piṉvarumāṟu eḻuti yaṉuppiṉāḷ: tē eṉṉum yāṟṟiṉ pālam iṭintu viḻunta irāppoḻutil yāṉ taṉiyē eṉ aṟiyil uṭkārntiruntēṉ. appōtu eṉ makaḷ mēṉmettikkuc caṭutiyil ōṭivantu palar kūccaliṭum oli yiṭiveḷiyil eṉakkuk kēṭkiṉṟatu eṉṟāḷ. ak kūccaliṉāl ētō tikilāṉa nikaḻcci nērntirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟum. makkaḷ palar māṇṭirukkavēṇṭu meṉṟuṅ kūṟiṉāḷ. maṟunāṭkāliyiṟ pāl koṇṭu vanta piyaṉ eṅkaḷ vēlikkāriyiṭam tē yāṟṟiṉ pālam iṭintu viḻuntateṉak kūṟiṉāṉ. itaṉāl ap peṇmaṇi kaṇṭatu meyyeṉa uṟutippaṭṭatu.
aṭikkuṟippu
1.	Bromine.
13.  kaṉaviṟṟōṉṟum uṇarccikaḷ
iṉi, mēṟcoṉṉapaṭiyākavaṉṟi, uṟaṅkuṅkāṟ kaṉaviṉiṭattun tolivu nikaḻccikaḷ vantu tōṉṟutal uṇṭu. itaṟku uṇmiyāka nikaḻntatoṉṟi iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām; oru kappaṟṟalivar1 tām kaṇṭa kaṉavu nikaḻcciyip piṉvarumāṟu eḻutukiṟār.
1825 ām āṇṭu cūṉ 7 ām nāḷ iravu yāṉ oru kaṉavu kaṇṭēṉ. iraṇṭu pāymaram uḷḷa oru kappal uḷḷē taṇṇīrkoṇṭu mitakka atil āṇmakkaḷ palarum avariṭiyē kariya ōr āṇmakaṉum irukkak kaṇṭēṉ. maṟunāḷ iravilum yāṉ atē kaṉavik kaṇṭu eḻuntu eṉ tuṇivaṉi eḻuppi eṉatu kappaliṉ mēṟṟaṭṭiṉmēṟ pōyp pārkkac ceytēṉ. yāṉum aṉṟip pakalmuḻutum mēṟṟaṭṭilēyē yiruntēṉ. tirumpavum 9 ām nāḷ iravu atē kaṉavu tōṉṟiṟṟu. uṭaṉē eḻuntu eṉatu kappalit tiruppi vēṟoru mukamāyp pōkacceytēṉ. aṉṟu māli eṭṭu maṇikkuk kaṭumpuyaṟ kāṟṟum iṭiyum miṉṉalun tōṉṟiṉa; ataṉāṟ pāykaḷic curukkiṉēṉ. maṟunāṭpakal veḷiccan tōṉṟiṟṟu. iṉiya kāṟṟu vīciyatu. 10 ām nāṭ kāli 8 maṇikkuk kappali vēṟoru mukamāyt tiruppiṉēṉ. inta nērattil, eṉ kappalukku ētō iṭarvarap pōkiṟateṉṟu eṇṇi eṉmaṉaṅ kalavaram aṭintatu. eṉṉuṭiya kaṉavippaṟṟi eṉ kūṭa irunta tuṇivaṉukkum, payaṇakkārarkaḷukkun terivittēṉ. avarkaḷ ataṉi ilaṇṭaṉiluḷḷa tam vīṭṭārkkum oru kaḻakattārkkum eḻuti aṟivittārkaḷ.
oṉpatari maṇikku yāṅkaḷ kāliyuṇavu eṭukkiyil, mēṟṟaṭṭiṉmēl aluval pārppavar vāṉattil veḷiccaṅ kuṟintu oru puyaṟkāṟṟu varukiṟateṉṟu kūviṉār. uṭaṉē yāṉ mēṟṟaṭṭiṟkup pōyc ciṟiya pāykaḷic curukkiṉēṉ. piṉ aṇiyattiṉ pakkamāyk kāṟṟu varuvataṉi yāṅkaḷ nōkkukiyil oru paṭakiṉiyum ataṉ tuṭuppiṉmēl oru peruṅkoṭiyip paṟakkaviṭṭuk koṇṭu ataṉip piṭittapaṭiyāy oruvaṉ ap paṭakiṉ kōṇattil niṟpatiyuṅ kaṇṭōm. uṭaṉē yāṉ kappali niṟuttip pāykaḷic curuṭṭiviṭṭēṉ. uyarap pāymarattiṉmēliruntu perumpāykaḷic curuṭṭiya eṉ āṭkaḷ tām palarip pārppatākavum, avarkaḷ kaṭaṟkaḷvar kūṭṭamā yirukkalāmeṉṟu niṉippatākavuṅ kūṟiṉārkaḷ. tāṉ atē kaṭaliṟ, ceṉṟa āṇṭu kaṭaṟkaḷvariṭam akappaṭṭukkoṇṭatu avvaḻiyē yeṉṟu eṉ āṭkaḷil oruvaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ. payaṇakkārarum eṉ kīḻ aluval pārppavaruṅ kappali vēṟorumukamāyt tiruppumpaṭi vaṟpuṟuttiyatōṭu, avarkaḷ kaṭaṟkaḷvarā
yiruntāl avarkaḷāl varuṅkēṭṭukku īṭuvāṅkuvatum muṭiyāmaṟpōm eṉṟum eṭutturittārkaḷ. ākavē, yāṉ kappaṟ pāykaḷi maṭittuk kaṭṭik kappalit tiruppi viṭṭuk kāliyuṇavukoḷḷak kīḻ iṟaṅkiṉēṉ. yāṉ eṉatu ciṟṟaṟikkuc ceṉṟatum, eṉ maṉam mikavum amiti ilatāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl, yāṉ pēraṟikkut tirumpivantēṉ. uṭaṉē yāṉ kaṇṭa kaṉaviṉ nikaḻcci eṉ uḷḷattil valiya vantu pukuntatu. eṉ iraṇṭu kit tuppākkikaḷiyuṅ kivāḷiyum iraṇṭu pakkattum vittukkoṇṭu, mēṟṟaṭṭiṉmēṟ pōy eṉ āṭkaḷiyellām aḻittuk kappali niṟuttic cukkāṉip piṭittu ataṉi niṟuttac coṉṉēṉ. eṉkīḻ mutaṟ kaṇkāṇi paṭakik kīḻ iṟakkik koṇṭupōyt toliviṟ ṟōṉṟum paṭakiṉiyum āṭkaḷiyum pārkkumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭēṉ. ataṉpiṉ eṉ pīraṅkikaḷi maruntiṭṭu iṭikkavuṅ, kappaṟ pāykaḷi virittukkaṭṭi ap paṭakukaḷiṉ mukamāyc cellavuṅ kaṟpittēṉ. aṅṅaṉaṅ kāṇappaṭṭa ak kūṭṭattāriṭam yāṅkaḷ vantu cērukiyil, yāṉ aṉuppiya eṉ tuṇivaṉ aṅkuḷḷa paṭakiṉiṉṟu ataṉ talivaṉiyum āṭkaḷiyum akaṟṟit taṉ paṭakiṉuḷ ēṟṟak kaṇṭēṉ. ap paṭaku kuṟukkut taṭṭu variyil nīr niṟintu, oru periya cuṟāmīṉi iḻuttuk koṇṭiruntatu.
cellārṭu eṉṉum peyar vāynta ak kappaṟṟalivaṉiyum avaṉ āṭkaḷiyum eṅkaḷ kappaliṉmēl ēṟṟikkoṇṭōm. avar kaḷellārum mika melintiruntamiyāṟ pēcutaṟkum valivillā tavarkaḷāy iruntārkaḷ. āṉāl, avarkaḷiṟ kariya niṟamuṭiyaṉāyirunta ōr āḷmaṭṭuñ ciṟitu nalla niliyiliruntamiyāl avaṉiṭamiruntu piṉvaruñ ceytikaḷi yellāṅ kēṭṭuṇarntōm. amerikkāvi
liruntu kireṉeṭā eṉṉum iṭattiṟkuc cellum iraṇṭu pāymarak kappal oṉṟukku avarkaḷ uriyarāyk kāṇappaṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ cellum vaḻiyil tām aruntuvataṟkut taṇṇīr tamatu kappaliṟ celavāyviṭṭamiyālum, nīṇṭavaḻi cella vēṇṭiyiruntamiyālum iṭiyēyuḷḷa oru tīvil taṇṇīr eṭukkum poruṭṭup pōṉārkaḷ. appōtu kāṟṟiṉṟi amitiyā yiruntataṉāl, at tīvaṇṭi kappali naṅkūram pāycci niṟuttic ciṟu paṭaku oṉṟil iṟaṅki at tīviṉ kariyi nōkkip pōkalāṉārkaḷ. appōtu iḷaṅkāṟṟu oṉṟu vīcat tuvaṅkiyatu; ciṟitu nērattilellām appaṭaku taṉ kappalirunta mukamāka illāmal vēṟoru mukamāy iḻuttukkoṇṭu pōkalāyiṟṟu; appōtu nīrōṭṭamum mikavun kaṭumiyāy appaṭakiṉi iḻuttukkoṇṭu ōṭiṉamiyāl ataṉi valittup pārttup pārttuk kaṭiciyāy atiluḷḷavarkaḷ muṟṟum iḷippaṭintu pōṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷatu pārvi eṭṭakkūṭiya aḷavu pārkkiyil, tamatu kappal at tīvaṇṭi naṅkūram pāycciya iṭattilēyē niṟkavum, tām ēṟiyirukkum paṭakumaṭṭum ak kappaliyuṅ kariyiyum vara vara akaṉṟu kaṭiciyāy avi kaṇṇukkup pulappaṭāta toliviṟ pōyviṭavum maṉamaṭivoṭu kaṇṭārkaḷ. aṭuttanāḷ avarkaṭku mikavum pollāta kālamāyiṟṟu. ēṉeṉṟāl, aṉṟikkuk kaṭuṅkāṟṟoṉṟu vīciyatu; iṭiyum miṉṉaluṅ kiḷarntaṉa; tuṭuppiṉiyum, pāymarattiṉiyum, pāykaḷiyum ap paṭakiṉ vaṭakkayiṟṟoṭu cērttuk kaṭṭiviṭṭu ōṭuṅkaṭal nīr ōṭṭattiṉ appaṭaku ūṭaṟuttuc cellumpaṭi taṉ pōkkilē ataṉi viṭṭuviṭṭārkaḷ. innēramellām avarkaḷ parukuvataṟkut taṇṇīrum illi, uṇpataṟku uṇavum illi. ataṟku aṭutta nāḷ kāṟṟu ēṟakkuṟiya amitiyuṟṟatu. ciṟu kāṟṟu aṭittālum, veḷiyil veppamō tīyttuviṭuṅ kaṭumiyuṭiyatāyiruntatu. aṉṟip pakalellām avarkaḷ paṭṭatuṉpam aḷaviṭappaṭātatu. anta mīkāmaṉum, avaṉ āṭkaḷun tāṅkaḷ uṭuttiyirunta tuṇikaḷik kiḻittuk kuḷirntirukkum poruṭṭut taṇṇīri mēlē ūṟṟik koṇṭārkaḷ. nāṉ avarkaḷip pōyk kaṇṭa aṉṟik kāliyil antak kariya āḷ taṉ kaṭavuḷi nōkkik kariya makkaḷ muṟiyīṭṭiṉiyum veḷḷimakkaḷ muṟiyīṭṭiṉiyuṅ kaṭavuḷ kēṭṭatu uṇṭēl, yāṅkaḷ iṟantu pōkāmip poruṭṭu eṅkaḷ uṇavukkāka oru mīṉi allatu cuṟāvi avar aruḷkūrntu! viṭukka eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉāṉ. ellā irakkamum uṭiya appaṉ avaṉatu vēṇṭukōḷic ceviyēṟṟu, avarkaḷiṭam oru periya cuṟā mīṉi viṭuppa aḵtu ap paṭakiṉmēṟ kiṭantatu; avarkaḷ ataṉ irattattik kuṭittu, ataṉ taciyilum oru pakutiyit tiṉṟārkaḷ. uṭaṉē yāṉ oru taṇṇīrp pattalit tiṟappittu avarkaḷi atil muḻukumpaṭi ceytēṉ. iṉṉum avarkaḷik kuḷirap paṇṇutaṟkum, veḷiyil veppattālum upput taṇṇī rāluṅ koppuḷittuppōṉa avarkaḷuṭampiṉ tōli āṟṟutaṟkum avarkaḷ mutukiṉ ṟōliṉmēl oṭṭik koṇṭirunta caṭṭit tuṇikaḷip piritteṭuttaṟkum, avarkaḷit taṉittaṉiyē taṇṇīrp pattalil talimuḻuku vittēṉ. ataṉpiṉ, avarkaḷ vāykaḷil īram uṇṭākum poruṭṭuc ciṟitu nērattiṟ korukāl tēttaṇṇīr parukakkoṭuttēṉ. avarkaḷiṟ cilar uṇavuñ cilar kōtumi yappattuṇṭum vēṇṭik kēṭṭārkaḷ. yāṉ avarkaṭku aricik kañciyum vāṟkōtumik kañciyum iṭikkiṭiyē koṭuttēṉ. naṉṟāyt tūṅkiyeḻunta piṟaku, antac cellārṭu eṉṉum mīkāmaṉ eṉṉoṭu pēcal vēṇṭi eṉṉik kīḻē varumpaṭi aḻittār. antak kariya āṇmakaṉ colliyavi yellām uṇmitāmā eṉṟu terintukoḷḷum poruṭṭu avartam caṭṭippikaḷi ārāyntu pārkkavē, avaratu kappaṟcarakkukaḷ patinta puttakamum aṭṭavaṇiyum akappaṭṭaṉa. aviyellām uṇmiyē eṉavum, avarkaḷ kaṭaṟkaḷva rallareṉavun terintu makiḻntēṉ. nāṅkaḷ iruvēmuṅ kalantu pēciyatiliruntu 1814 ām āṇṭu niyūpavuṇṭilāntil yōvāṉ tirukkōyilil avarum yāṉum muṉṉamē oruvariyoruvar kaṇṭu pēciṉami niṉivukku vantatu. appōtē avar iraṇṭu pāymarak kappaloṉṟiṟkut talivarā yiruntār.
uṇmiyāka nikaḻnta ik kaṉavunikaḻcciyil, oru kappaṟ ṟalivaṟkum avaṉṟaṉ āṭkaṭkun tolivil nērnta iṭariṉ vakikaḷ avvaḷavum maṟṟoru kappaṟṟalivaṟku aṭuttaṭuttut tōṉṟi, avaṉ avar tamakku utavi ceyyumāṟu ēviya taṉmi peritum niṉivu kūraṟpāṟṟu. nalla eṇṇam vāynta ak kappaṟṟalivaṉ uḷḷamē tolivil nērnta  avviṭariṉik kaṉaviṉkaṭ kaṇṭuṇarntatō, allatu kaṭavuḷē at tōṟṟattiṉik kaṉaviṉkaṇ avaṉukku eḻuppi avaṉ utavi ceyyumāṟu avaṉi ēviṉarō nām avvakiyiṉi aṟiyēm. tolivil nērnta iṭar, ataṉic ciṟitumē yaṟiyāta oru mīkāmaṉukkuk kaṉaviṉkaṭ ṭāṉē vantu pulap paṭṭamitāṉ iṅkē karutaṟpāṟṟu.
iṉip, piṉṉē nikaḻappōkum nikaḻccikaḷ muṉṉē kaṉaviṉkaṇ vantu tōṉṟutalum uṇṭu. ataṉi meyp pittaṟku uṇmiyā nikaḻnta cila nikaḻccikaḷi iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām;
ceṉṟa iravu tam naṇparkaḷ tammip pārkka vantapōtu pirāṭṭu eṉpavar tām muntiṉa iraviṟ kaṇṭa kaṉānikaḻcciyi eṭutturikkalāṉār. tām iṟantupōṉatākavun, tām nalla tūkkattiliruntālun tammic cuṟṟi naṭappatavvaḷavun tāṅ kāṇak kūṭiyatāyiruntatākavum, āṉāl appōtu tām pēcavāvatu appuṟam ippuṟan tirumpavāvatu māṭṭāmaliruntatākavum oru kaṉavu kaṇṭār; tammi aṭakkam paṇṇuvataṟku vēṇṭum ēṟpāṭukaḷ ceyyap paṭṭatiyuñ, cavappeṭṭiyit tāṅkap pōkum āṟu nēcarkaḷiṉ peyar iṉṉateṉpatiyum avar kuṟippiṭṭuc coṉṉār. avaṟṟoṭu tammi aṭakkañ ceyyap pōkum iṭattiyun, tammi vikkuṅ kallaṟiyiṉiyum, ak kallaṟikkuḷ tamatu cavappeṭṭiyit tāḻtti iṟakkumāṟṟiyum ellām piḻipaṭāmal eṭuttuk kūṟiṉār. piṟaku tamatu cavappeṭṭimēl mutalil veṭṭit taḷḷiya maṇ viḻuntatun tām at tūkkattiṉiṉṟum viḻittukkoṇṭatākavun kūṟiṉār. avar iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṇṭu kūṟiya kaṉaviṉpaṭiyē maṟunāḷil iṟantupōṉār. tām iravu paṉṉiraṇṭu maṇikku iṟappatākavum uṟuti coṉṉār. ellām avar kaṇṭapaṭiyē niṟivēṟiṉa. avar kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭa āṟu nēcarkaḷē avaratu cavaccaṭaṅki niṟivēṟṟiṉar.
maṟṟoruvar piṉṉē naṭakkap pōvataṉi muṉṉē kaṇṭatuṅ, kaṇṭapaṭiyē naṭantatum uyirnili ārāyccik kaḻakattārkku eḻutitterivikka, ak kaḻakattār avar kūṟiyati ārāyntu pārttu ēṟṟuk koṇṭārākaliṉ, ataṉiyum iṅkē eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām:
mutalāka eṉ uṭampu cevviyāṉa nilimiyilirukkiṟa teṉpataṉit terivikkiṉṟēṉ. maṭattaṉamāṉa nampikki eṉṉiṭañ ciṟitum illi. poyyāṉa  tōṟṟaṅkaḷukku nāṉ uṭpaṭṭatumilli. attakiyavaṟṟi ārāyvatil eṉakkuk karuttuc celvatumilli. mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ, appōtu patiṉeṭṭu āṇṭuḷḷa eṉ makaṉ aṭuttuḷḷa mākāṇattil oru maruntuk kaṭiyil aluvalil amartaṟkuc ceṉṟāṉ. avaṉ pōvataṟku yāṉ muḻutum uṭaṉpaṭṭu viṭikoṭutta tallāmal, avaṉip paṟṟiyāṉ oru ciṟitum maṉam varuntavilli. ceṉṟa vēṉiṟ kālattiṟ kiḻakkē nīṇṭa payaṇam pōyc cila kālam aṅkiruntēṉ. yāṉ vīṭṭil illāta poḻutu, eṉ makaṉip paṟṟiyāvatu eṉ kuṭumpattār evarip paṟṟiyāvatu yāṉ ciṟitum kavali yaṭiyavilli. maṉak kiḷarcciyōṭiruppatē eṉatu iyaṟki. āṉāṟ, ceṉṟa iliyutir kālattil, tiṭṭamāy iṉṉateṉṟu colla muṭiyā vakiyāy eṉ makaṉippaṟṟi maṉakkuṟi koḷḷa vāṉēṉ. maṉattuyaram aṭintēṉeṉṟu collal muṭiyātu; āṉāl, etaṉāl eṉṟum eṉṉāṟ collal muṭiyātu. maṉakkuṟi maṭṭum uṟṟēṉ. avaṉ vīṭṭil illāta mūṉṟu āṇṭukaḷilum yāṉ eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷik kāṭṭilum pala kaṭitaṅkaḷ avaṉukku eḻutiṉēṉ. navampar muṟpakutiyil avaṉ eṅkaḷik kāṇa vīṭṭiṟku vantāṉ. avaṉ tirumpa eṅkaḷi viṭṭuc ceṉṟa piṟaku, avaṉippaṟṟi eṉakku muṉṉiyilum mikka varuttamum maṉak kuṟiyum uṇṭāyiṉa. āṉāl, aḵtu ētō iṭar varappōkiṟa teṉpatik kuṟitta maṉakkalavaramum aṉṟu, accamum aṉṟu. avaṉippaṟṟi eviyō maṉakkuṟiyum varuttamuntām. eṉ uḷḷa nikaḻcciyi yāṉē eṭutturikkak kūṭavilli. eṉṉāl niṉivukūrak kūṭiyavariyil, yāṉ aṟacciṟu piyaṉāyiruntapōtu, eṉ takappaṉār iṟantataṉ piṟaku yāṉ eṅṅaṉam maṉam nekiḻntēṉō aṅṅaṉamē ippōtum maṉam nekiḻappeṟṟēṉ. eṉvāḻkkiyiṉ oḷi eṉṉi viṭṭuc caṭutiyil akaṉṟupōyateṉavum, iṉi yāṉ uyirōṭiruppa taṟku oṉṟumilliyeṉavum ōreṇṇan tōṉṟiyatu. tirikikkalli yotta oru niṟi eṉatu uyiri nacukkuvatu pōṟ kāṇappaṭṭatu. uyir vāḻkkiyil eṉakkuc ciṟitum viruppamilliyeṉṟu eṉ naṇparkaṭkup palakāṟ collivantēṉ. ṭicampart tiṅkaḷ cellac cella ivvuṇarcci eṉakku mikuntuvaralāyiṟṟu; eppaṭiyō eṉ makaṉē ataṟku naṭukkuṟiyākat tōṉṟiṉaṉ. iraviṟ palakāl avaṉi niṉittapaṭiyāy eḻuntiruppēṉ. uyir vāḻkkiyiṉ veṟumiyiṉiyum pōlit taṉmiyiṉiyum āḻa niṉittalāl yāṉ tūṅkamuṭivatilli. ṭicampart tiṅkaḷ pattoṉpatām nāṭkāliyiṟ ciṟitu muṉṉē tuyil nīṅki eḻuntēṉ. ētōtattāṉa nilimikku yāṉ varuvatākat tōṉṟiṟṟu. evariyum eḻuppāmal yāṉē ceṉṟu neruppu mūṭṭiṉēṉ. eṉ vāḻnāṭ kālattil iṅṅaṉam yāṉē itaṟkumuṉ ceytatilli. an neruppaṇṭiyil uṭkārntukoṇṭu niṉittup pārkkap pukuntēṉ. eṉṉi varuttum accamuḷḷa tuṉpam iṉṉateṉṟu eṉṉāl aṟiyak kūṭavilli. aḵtiṉṉateṉṟu terintilatu. eṉakkuttāṉ yāteṉṟun teriyavilli, iṉṉum eṉmakaṉukkuttāṉ ētēṉun tuṉpamō iṭarō varappōkiṟateṉṟum yāṉ tuṇiyak kūṭavilli. kāli ēḻumaṇikku eṉ maṉivi tuyil nīṅki yeḻuntu paṭukkimēl uṭkārntu, taṉ uḷḷatti mika uṟuttiyatoru kaṉavu kaṇṭatākak kūṟiṉāḷ; ak kaṉavutāṉ avaḷi viḻikkac ceytatu.
avaḷ kūṟiyatāvatu; yāṉ itaṟkumuṉ pārttaṟiyāta makkaḷiṭiyē oru putiya iṭattil nīṅkaḷ iruppatāka eṉakkup paṭṭatu. paḷḷikkūṭattiṟkuc celluñ ciṟu piḷḷikaḷ palarum paruvam vantu vaḷarnta oru peṇpiḷḷiyum uṭiya oru peruṅ kuṭumpamāy atu kāṇappaṭṭatu. nāṉ anta iṭattiṟku oru carakku vaṇṭiyil vantēṉ; āṉāl, nīṅkaḷō eṉakku muṉṉamē aṅku vantiruntīrkaḷ. nīṅkaḷ antak kuṭumpat tāroṭu mikavum nerukkamāṉa paḻakkamuṭiyīrāyk kāṇappaṭṭīrkaḷ. antap periya peṇṇāṉavaḷ nuṅkaḷ maṭimēl uṭkārntu taṉ kikaḷāl uṅkaḷ kaḻuttic cuṟṟikkoṇṭu aṭuttaṭuttu uṅkaḷi muttamiṭṭatu. attuṇi nerukkamāy uṟavukoḷḷumpaṭi nīṅkaḷ inta makkaḷi eṅkē kaṇṭu piṭittīrkaḷeṉṟu yāṉ viyantu koṇṭirukkiyil, nīṅkaḷ caṭutiyiṟ kīḻviḻuntu iṟantu pōṉīrkaḷ. nāṉum uṭaṉē viḻittukkoṇṭēṉ; eṉpatēyām.
intak kaṉavu uṇmiyākavē naṭipeṟumōveṉṟu maṉakkalakkaṅ koṇṭēṉ. yāṉ avvaḷavu tuṉpuṟṟa nilimiyilirukkiṉṟēṉeṉṟum uvālṭarippaṟṟi yāṉ mikavum varuntutalāl eṉatu uyir vāḻakki eṉakkē oru cumiyāy irukkiṟateṉṟum yāṉ avaḷukku maṟumoḻi kūṟiṉēṉ. kāliyuṇavu koṇṭapiṉ, eṉ makaḷik kūppiṭṭu uvālṭarukku oru kaṭitam eḻuti uṭaṉē avaṉ vīṭṭukku varumpaṭi col eṉṟēṉ. ak kaṭitaṅ kaṭṭāyam pōyc cērumpaṭi ataṉi mutal vaṇṭikkē avaḷ viṭukkumāṟuñ  ceytēṉ. ataṉpiṉ maṉakkiḷarcciyiṉ poruṭṭu eṉatu vayalnilattip pārkkak kutirimēṟ ceṉṟēṉ. āṉāṟ, pakal 12 maṇikkellām vanta ceytiyāṉatu eṉ makaṉ kaṭuṅkāyam aṭintu uṇarviṉṟik kiṭakkiṉṟāṉeṉa aṟivittatu. uṭaṉē eṉ maṉiviyiyum makaḷiyum piṉvaṇṭiyil varukaveṉṟu colliviṭṭu, yāṉ carakkuvaṇṭiyil ēṟi eṉ makaṉ anniliyiṟ kiṭakkum iṭattiṟkuc ceṉṟēṉ. ippōtu ap putumiyāṉa nikaḻcciyil uṉṉikkat takkatoru kuṟipput tōṉṟalāyiṟṟu. ētō oru piḻipāṭṭāl eṉ maṉiviyum makaḷum pukivaṇṭiyit tavaṟaviṭṭu, oru nāṭṭu vaṇṭiyil ēṟi nērēvantu cērntārkaḷ. vaḻiyil iṭiyiṭiyē ovvoru ciṟṟūriluṅ kutirikaḷ māṟṟa vēṇṭi yiruntamiyāl, avarkaḷ eṉ makaṉiṭam iravu 11 maṇikkuttāṉ vantu cērntārkaḷ. it tuyaranikaḻcci nērnta iṭam mikac ciṟanta oru kuṭiyāṉavariṉ illattiṉ arukāmiyilākum; ēṉeṉṟāl, ik kuṭiyāṉavariṉ makaḷi eṉ makaṉ  neṭunāḷākap pōyp pārppatu vaḻakkam. ivar avaṉukkuc ciṟanta naṇparākiyālum, ivaratu illam arukē yiruntamiyālum avaṉi avviṭattiṟkē koṇṭu ceṉṟārkaḷ. avaratu  periya ak kuṭumpattiṉ piḷḷikaḷ ellārum eṉ makaṉiṭattē mikkatōr aṉpu vittiruntārkaḷ. eṉ makaṉ iṟantatippaṟṟi avarkaḷ aṭinta tuyaratti viṭa nāṅkaḷ mikutiyāyt tuyaruṟṟōmeṉṟu eṉṉāl uṇmiyākavē collal muṭiyātu. eṉ makaṉ uṇarviṉṟik kiṭanta avvaṟiyiṉuḷ eṉ maṉiyāḷ nuḻinta pōtu, yāṉ muṉṉē kuṟippiṭṭa ak kuṭiyāṉavariṉ makaḷ,  eṉ makaṉatu paṭukkiyiṉ talimāṭṭil aḻutapaṭiyāy niṉṟukoṇṭiruntāḷ. eṉ maṉavi avviṭattic cuṟṟilum orumuṟi nōkkiyatum, itu tāṉ yāṉ kaṇṭa kaṉavu! nīṅkaḷ irukkak kaṇṭa aṟiyum itu tāṉ ! yāṉ kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭa makkaḷum ivarkaḷ tām! eṉṟu eṉ kātiṟ kucukucu veṉak kūṟiṉāḷ. avaḷ nāṭṭu vaṇṭiyil vanta ceytiyum avaḷ kaṇṭa kaṉaviṉi meyppaṭuttiyatu. ak kuṭumpattiṉarum avaḷ colliya aṭiyāḷappaṭiyē yiruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kaḷḷam illāta mikac ciṟanta kuṭimakkaḷāvar. mēlum, avaḷ neṭukap pārttu vanta nāṭṭiṉ iyalpum, iṟutiyāka avvillattic cūḻa avaḷ kaṇṭa iyaṟkittōṟṟamun tāṉ kaṇṭa kaṉavil tōṉṟiyapaṭiyē muḻutum ottiruntaṉa.
muṭivāka yāṉ collaṟpālatu, eṉṉi atukāṟum peritun tuṉpuṟuttivanta at tuyaraniṉivu eṉṉi viṭṭu akaṉṟatēyākum. eṉ uyiriyē nacukki viṭuvatā yirunta collutaṟkākā ap peruntuyarc cumiyiṉi ṭicampar 17 ām nāṭ kāliyiliruntu yāṉ uṇaravilli. eṉ makaṉi iḻanta tuyaram eṉakku uḷateṉṟālum. iḵtu yāṉ muṉṉaṭinta tuyarattiṉ vēṟāvatākum1
eṉappōnta kaṉavu nikaḻcciyil iṟantupaṭṭa taṉmakaṉukku vēṟākat taṉ kaṇavaṉiyē avvāṟu avvammiyār kaṇṭa oru piḻiyit tavira, maṟṟiyavellām ak kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭapaṭiyē oruṅkottiruttal perituṅ karuttiṟ patikkaṟ pālatākum.
iṉṉum, iḻantupōṉa poruḷkaḷit tirumpap peṟutaṟku evvaḷavō muyaṉṟum avaṟṟik kaṇṭu piṭikka māṭṭāmal mika varuntik kaḷittup paṭuttu uṟaṅkiṉavar cilar avi irukkumiṭaṅkaḷik kaṉaviṟkaṇṭu viḻittup, piṉṉar avviṭaṅkaḷip pōyp pārttu, avi tammik kaṇṭeṭutta varalāṟukaḷum pala uḷḷaṉa. avi tammuḷ, aṟiñar cilarāl ārāyntu uṇmiyeṉat teḷiyappaṭṭa cilavaṟṟimaṭṭum iṅku eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām:
orukāl vaḻakkaṟiñar oruvar iravu kaṭitaṅkaḷ eḻutat tuvaṅki, muṭittapiṉ avaṟṟip paṉṉiraṇṭari maṇikku añcaṟ peṭṭiyil iṭa veḷiyē ceṉṟār; ceṉṟu tirumpiyavuṭaṉ tam uṭikaḷik kaḻaṟṟukiyiṟ karuvūlattil (Bank) tām oru peruntoki vāṅkik koḷḷumpaṭi, muṉ nāḷiṟ kuṟittuk koṭukkappaṭṭa oru cīṭṭi (Cheque) evvāṟō tām iḻantu viṭṭamikaṇṭār. uṭaṉē ataṉi evvaḷavō ovvōriṭattun tēṭip pārttum, aḵtu akappaṭṭilatu. ataṉ mēl avar maṉattuyarat tōṭum paṭukkikkuc ceṉṟu uṟaṅkiṉār; aṅṅaṉam uṟaṅkukiyil tamatu vīṭṭu vāyilukku neṭuntolivil allāta vēliyaṭippuḷḷatōr iṭattil accīṭṭuc curuḷāyk kiṭakkak kaṉavu kaṇṭār. kaṇṭu viḻittatum, eḻuntu āṭi yaṇintu puṟattē teru vaḻiyē ēkit, tām kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭa avviṭattilēyē antac cīṭṭuc curuḷāyk kiṭattalik kaṇṭu, ataṉi makiḻntu eṭuttukkoṇṭār.
maṟṟorukāl oru perumāṭṭi taṉatu vīṭṭukku aṇmiyilirunta oru kāṭṭukkuḷ ulavappōṉāḷ; pōyt tirumpi vantatum avaḷ tāṉ vittirunta oru mutaṉmiyāṉa cāviyik kāṇāmaṟ kalaṅkiṉāḷ. tirumpip pōyt tēṭalāmeṉṟāl mālip poḻutupōy iruṇṭuviṭṭatu. ataṉmēl avaḷ maṉak kavaliyuṭiyaḷāy uṟaṅkac ceṉṟāḷ. avvāṟu avaḷ uṟaṅkukiyil tāṉ ulavivanta ak kāṭṭiṉkaṇ uḷḷa oru marattiṉ vēr aṇṭiyil accāvi kiṭappatākak kaṉavu kaṇṭāḷ. maṟunāṭ kāliyil avaḷ akkāṭṭiṉuṭ pōyt, tāṉ kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭa aṭiyāḷappaṭiyirunta oru marattiṉ vēraṇṭiyiṟ ceṉṟu pārkka, ac cāvi aṅkē kiṭantatu; uṭaṉē makiḻnteṭuttuk koṇṭāḷ.
iṉṉuṅ, kāṇāmaṟpōṉa oru peṇṇiṉ cavatti maṟṟoru peṇ tāṉ kaṇṭa kaṉaviṉ vāyilākak kaṇṭaṟinta varalāṟṟiṉiyum iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām:
ṣāṅkaliṉ eṉṉum ūriṟ paṭittalivarāṉa lumṭaṉ (Lumsden) eṉpavariṉ makaḷāṉa mārjori eṉpavaḷ 1903 ām āṇṭu pipravarit tiṅkaḷ 10 ām nāḷ naṭipeṟṟa nāṭakam oṉṟil tāṉuñ cērntu āṭiṉavaḷ, aṉṟikku eppaṭiyō māyamāyk kāṇāmaṟ pōyviṭṭāḷ. atupaṟṟip peruntuyarkoṇṭa avaḷ tantiyār, tam makaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittut tarupavarkku 50 pavuṉ paricu aḷippatāka viḷamparappaṭutti yiruntār. appōtu cilakempu eṉṉum oru cempaṭamātu ōriravu tāṉ uṟaṅkukiyil taṉakkut tōṉṟiya kaṉavil ōriḷam paruvattu maṅki kaṭaṟkarit tuṟimukap pālattil ulāvik koṇṭirunta nērattiṟ kāltavaṟik kaṭalil vīḻntamiyum, vīḻnta appeṇṇiṉ cavam ōriṭattil otuṅkik kiṭappatiyun teḷivāyk kaṇṭāḷ. kaṇṭu viḻittavaḷ maṟunāṭ kāliyil (atāvatu pipravari 20 ām nāḷ viṭiyalil) tāṉ kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭa iṭattip pōyp pārkka, meyyākavē oru peṇ piṇamāyk kiṭappati nōkkit tiṭukkiṭṭu, ūravarel lārkkum acceytiyi ōṭivantu aṟivittāḷ. uṭaṉē ap peṇṇiṉ tantiyārum ūrkāvaṟṟalivarum ap piṇattaṇṭi vantu cērntu, avaḷatu cāviṉ uṇmiyiṉi ārāykiyil, ak kaṭaṟ kāvalāḷiyoruvar appattām nāḷ iraviṟ patiṉoru maṇikkuc ciṟitu muṉṉē oru maṅki pālattiṉmēl niṉṟu koṇṭiruntataṉit tām pārttu viṭṭu appāṟpōyp, pattunimiṭaṅ kaḻintapiṉ mīṇṭum avviṭatti vantu nōkka am maṅkiyār aṅku ilarāyiṉār eṉavum, ataṉ mēl am maṅki eṅkē ceṉṟirukkak kūṭumeṉṟu terital vēṇṭi appālattē otuṅkutaṟkeṉṟu amikkappaṭṭa ciṟu koṭṭakikaḷit tām pārttum avaṟṟuḷḷum avar illāmiyāl irāccāppāṭu koṇṭapiṉ appālattē ulāvavanta am maṅkiyār tirumpip pōyirukkalā meṉṟeṇṇit tām ētuṅ kalavarañ ceyyavilli eṉavuñ cāṉṟu moḻintār. ataṉpiṉ, ac cempaṭavamātu ap peṇṇiṉ piṇattik kaṇṭupiṭittatu evvāṟu eṉṟu avarkaḷ avaḷi viṉava, avaḷ annikaḻcciyit tāṉ taṉatu kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭatum, ak kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭa iṭattip pōyp pārkka ap peṇ piṇamāyk kiṭantatum ākiya varalāṟukaḷi yeṭutturit tāḷ. avaṟṟik kēṭṭu iṟumpūtuṟṟa tantiyār avaṭku aimpatupavuṉ paricu koṭuttār eṉpatu.
iṉi, iṟakkun taṟuvāyil uḷḷavarkaḷ, tamakku uṟaviṉā rāyuḷḷa evarmāṭṭu aṉpu mikutiyāy vittirukkiṉṟār kaḷō avari annērattil niṉittalum avarik kāṇa virumputalum uṭiyarāy iṟantupaṭuvatu vaḻakkam. uyir uṭampiviṭṭup piriyum annērattil niṉitta niṉivum virumpiya viruppamum vīṇāyp pōvatilli. an niṉiviṉ uṟippukkum viruppattiṉ aḻuttattiṟkum ēṟṟapaṭi, niṉikkap paṭṭavarkku iṟantupaṭuvāriṉ uruvamum avar iṟantu paṭu nikaḻcciyuṅ kaṉaviṉkaṇ vantu tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. itaṟku uṇmiyāka nikaḻnta cila varalāṟukaḷi īṇṭeṭuttuk kāṭṭutum:
ōr ūril ōr ammiyārukku iraṇṭu putalviyar iruntaṉar. avar avviruvariyum avvūrilēyē maṇañceytu koṭuttiruntaṉar. avvammiyār iṭiyiṭiyē ceṉṟu kaṇavar illattilirukkun tam putalviyar iruvariyum pārttu aḷavaḷāva varutal vaḻakkam. aṅṅaṉamē oru nāḷ mālik kālattil avvammiyār tam putalviyaruṭaṉ aḷavaḷāviyiruntu viṭṭuc, ciṟitu toliviluḷḷa tamatu illattiṟkut tirumpip pōyiṉār. ap putalvimār iruvarum tattam illil iravuṇā arunti uṟaṅkac ceṉṟaṉar. ellārum ayarntuṟaṅkum naḷḷiravil ōr illil irunta orutti pēraccattoṭu veruṇṭeḻuntu, taṉ kaṇavaṉik kūviyeḻuppi, uṭaṉē yāṉ eṉ aṉṉiyiṭañ cellal vēṇṭum; ātalāl vaṇṭi koṇṭuvarac colluṅkaḷ eṉ aṉṉiyār mikavum nōyppaṭṭirukkiṉṟāreṉṟu uṟutiyāy nampukiṉṟēṉ eṉṟāḷ. atukēṭṭa avaḷ kaṇavaṉ nī kaṇṭatu veṟuṅkaṉavē yākiyāl ataṟkāka ivvaḷavu kalakkam vēṇṭām eṉa evvaḷavō vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟiyum, avaḷ amitiyaṭiyāmiyāl, taṉatu vaṇṭiyi varuvittāṉ. avaḷum ataṉkaṇ amarntu tam aṉṉiyiṉ vīṭṭukku arukē celkiyil iraṇṭu pāṭṭikaḷ oṉṟu cērumiṭattil taṉ tamakkiyiṉ vaṇṭiyum varak kaṇṭāḷ. kaṇṭu annērattil ēṉ varalvēṇṭum? eṉa oruvari
yoruvar viṉaviyapōtu, yāṉ uṟaṅkak kūṭavilli, nam aṉṉiyār nōyppaṭṭirukkiṉṟār eṉṉum uṟutiyiṉāl, yāṉ avarip pārkkavantēṉ eṉṟu iruvarum oruvarukkoruvar collik koṇṭaṉar. piṉṉar avviru varun tam aṉṉiyiṉ vīṭṭaṇṭi vantatun, tam aṉṉikku nampikkiyuḷḷa vēlikkāri av vīṭṭu vāyililē niṟkak kaṇṭārkaḷ. kiṭṭac ceṉṟatum, avaḷ avviruvariyum nōkki nuṅkaḷ aṉṉiyār tiṭīreṉṟu nōyppaṭṭu iṟantupōkum niliyil irukkiṉṟār; nuṅkaḷ iruvariyum pārkkavēṇṭu meṉat tamatu viruppattitterivittār, eṉṟu colliṉaḷ. uṭaṉē uḷ nuḻintu avarkaḷ tam aṉṉiyiṉ arukē cella, avvammi avarkaḷik kaṇ tiṟantu pārttu maṉa niṟivōṭu uyir tuṟantār.
iṉṉum, 1857 ām āṇṭu mārccut tiṅkaḷ 21 ām nāḷukkumuṉ nikaḻnta oru kaṉavu nikaḻcciyum iṅkē kuṟikkattakkatā yirukkiṉṟatu. ilantaṉmā nakarattilirunta meṉṉiyar eṉṉum ammiyār tam uṭaṉ piṟantā ṉoruvaṉiṭattu nirampiya aṉpuṭiyarāyiruntaṉar. eṉṟālum,  pala āṇṭukaḷāka avaṉ irukkumiṭamum avaṉippaṟṟiya varalāṟum ētumē avaḷ aṟintu koḷḷak kūṭavilli. ivvāṟirukka, mārccut tiṅkaḷil orunāḷ, avaḷ, taṉṉuṭaṉpiṟantāṉ taṉ paṭukkiyaṇṭi taliyaṟṟa muṇṭamāy niṟkavum, avaṉatu tali avaṉ arukēyirunta cavappeṭṭiyiṉuḷḷē kiṭakkavum oru pollāta kaṉavu kaṇṭu, pēraccamum peruntuyaramum oruṅku kalanta uḷḷattiṉaḷāy viḻittukkoṇṭāḷ. uṭaṉē tāṉ kaṇṭa ak koṭiya kaṉaviṉ ceytiyip palarkkut terivittatoṭu putiṉattāḷ kaḷilun taṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāṉākiya uvilliṅṭaṉippaṟṟi evarēṉun terinteḻutumāṟum viḷamparañ ceytiruntaṉaḷ. avvāṟu ceytatiliruntu, cāravākku eṉṉum ūrilē avaṉ cīṉarkaḷāl tali tuṇikkappaṭṭu iṟantāṉ eṉṉuñ ceyti veḷiyāyiṟṟu. avvāṟu avaṉ tali veṭṭuṇṭa atēnāḷiṟṟāṉ avaṉṟaṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāḷāṉa avvammi akkaṉavuṅ kaṇṭami piṉṉar aṟiñar ceyta ārāycciyāṟ pulaṉāyiṟṟu.
ivviruvēṟu uṇmi nikaḻccikaḷi liruntu peṟappaṭuvatu yātu? aṉpu mikkār iruvarō palarō aṇmiyil akaṉṟiruppiṉum allatu cēymiyil akaṉṟiruppiṉum avaruḷ oruvar iṟakkuṅkāl, avar niṉitta niṉivāṉatu uṭaṉē tamakku aṉparāṉa ēṉiyōrkkum avaratu iṟappiṉ varalāṟṟiṉip piḻipaṭāmaṟ kaṉaviṉkaṇ aṟivikku meṉpatēyaṉṟō?
iṉi, iṟappavarē paṉṟi nōyāyk kiṭappavaruntamakku utavi ceyvāri uṟuttu niṉikka, an niṉivu avarkku aṉparāyiṉār kaṉaviṉkaṭ ṭōṉṟi ataṉi aṟivikka, av aṉparum viḻitteḻuntu avarpāṟceṉṟu avarkku nōy nīkkiṉa varalāṟukaḷum mikappala; avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu iṅkē kāṭṭuvām; kaṭpulaṉākātavaṟṟik kāṇṭal1 eṉṉum ariyanūliṉ āciriyarāṉa jēm kōṭ eṉpavar piṉvarumāṟu eḻutukiṉṟār:
yāṉ eṉ kuṭumpattavaruṭaṉ kiḷākō eṉṉum nakarattil taṅkiyirukkuṅkāl,  ṭōṉālṭmākkiṉṉaṉ eṉṉuṅ kiṟittucamaya kuru ematu vīṭṭukku ayalil uḷḷatōr illattiṟ kuṭiyiruntār. avarum nāṅkaḷum mikka nēyamuṭiyarāy oḻukiṉōm. avar tam maṉiviyār kālamākic cila āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintaṉa. avar tammāl iyaṉṟa maṭṭum vēlikkārari vittuk koṇṭu tamatu kuṭittaṉatti naṭattiyavāṟē tamakkuriya camayaviḷakkat toṇṭiyuñ cevviyāka āṟṟivantār.

muṉ tām pārāta ōr iḷiya mātu mikka maṉattuyarattoṭu pōntu ac camaya kuru mikavum nōyāyk kiṭappati vantu pārkkumpaṭi tammik kēṭṭuk koṇṭatākavum, ataṟ kicintu tām am mātarāruṭaṉ ceṉṟu ak kuruviṉip pārttatākavun tāṅ kaṇṭa kaṉaviṉ pakutikaḷi moḻintār. avaṟṟōṭu avar irunta uṭampiṉ niliyiṉiyum, avar paṭuttirunta aṟiyiṉ aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷiyun tāṅ kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭavāṟē viritturittār.
ak kaṉavil ētēṉum uḷatōveṉṟu yāṅkaḷ eṇṇik koṇṭirukkiyiṟ kuṟippiṭṭa av vaḻiyē evarō varum aravan tōṉṟiyatu; vāyil maṇiyum aṭi
paṭṭatu. eṅkaḷ vēlikkāri uṟaṅkappōy viṭṭamiyāl, eṉ maṉiviyē vāyilaṇṭi ceṉṟār; meyyākavē ōr iḷam peṇ vantu niṉṟāḷ; ivaḷ ak kuruviṉāṟ putitāka ēṟpaṭuttappaṭṭa vēlikkāri yākiyāl eṉ maṉivi ivaḷi itaṟkumuṟ pārttatē yilli; aṅṅaṉam vantu niṉṟa avviḷamātu, mikavum nōyāyk kiṭakkum mutiyōrāṉa ak kuruviṉi vantu pārkkum paṭi eṉ maṉiviyik keñcik kēṭṭukkoṇṭāḷ. eṉ maṉiviyuṭaṉē eṉṉik kūppiṭṭār; avviḷa maṅkiyiṉ ṟōṟṟamum uṭiyuṅ kaṉaviṉ kaṭ pōnta maṅkiyiṉ ṟōṟṟattiyum uṭiyiyum ottiruntaṉa. appoḻutē eṉ maṉivi avarip pārkkapōṉār; yāṉum ṭaṅkaṉ eṉṉum pukaḻpeṟṟa maruttuvar oruvari aḻikkac ceṉṟēṉ. am maruttuvar varutaṟkuc ciṟitu nēramākumeṉak kaṇṭamiyāl, yāṉ muṉṉatāka ak kuruviṉ illattiṟku ēkiṉēṉ; mēṉmettimēl avaratu aṟikkuḷ nuḻintatum, eṉ maṉiviyār tām ataṉik kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭu piṉ eṉakkuk kūṟiyapaṭiyē aḵtiruntatu. ciṟitu nērattil am maruttuvarum vantār. avar coṉṉapaṭiyē ellāñ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉavā eṉṟu pārttaṟkāka eṉ maṉiviyuṅ kūṭavē yiruntār. eṉ maṉivi kaṇṭa kaṉaviṉ ceytiyik kēṭṭa ammaruttuvar nakittu, attakiya nikaḻccikaḷ naṭakkak kūṭiyavitām eṉṟu moḻintār. ataṉpiṉ akkuruviṉ makaṉukkum marumakaḷukkuṅ kampicceyti viṭukka, avarkaḷum uṭaṉē vantu cērntaṉar. avarkaṭkum eṉ maṉivi kaṇṭa kaṉavu collappaṭṭatu. piṉṉar akkuruvum uṭampu nalam peṟṟār, eṉpatu.
camayattoṇṭu āṟṟuvārāṉa akkuru tām nōyāyk kiṭanta poḻutu tamakkutavi ceyvār evarum arukil illātu pōkavē, tamakku ayaliluḷḷa tamnēyarkaḷi avvutaviyiṉ poruṭṭu uṟuttu niṉikka, an niṉi vāṉatu, an nēyaril oruvarāṉa avvammiyāratu kaṉaviṉ kaṇ avartam putiya vēlikkāriyiṉ uruviṉiyum, avar nōyāyk kiṭakkum niliyiṉiyum naṉku viḷaṅkap pulappaṭutti, avvāṟṟāl avviruvarum uṭaṉē pōntu avarkku utavipuriyumāṟu ceytami kāṇka.
iṅṅaṉamē, pēriṭaril akappaṭṭuk koṇṭār palariṉ tōṟṟamāṉatu aruḷ uḷḷam uṭiyār cilariṉ kaṉaviṉkaṭ ṭōṉṟi, avaratu utaviyāl avarkaḷi avviṭariṉiṉṟum mīḷvitta uṇmi nikaḻccikaḷum palavuṇṭu; avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu iṅkē kāṭṭuvām.
kālipōrṉiyā eṉṉum ūrilirunta oru paṭittalivar, ayalnāṭu kuṭiyēṟap pukunta oru kūṭṭattār kuḷirālum paciyālun tuṉpuṟutali oru nāṭ kaṉaviṉ kaṭ kaṇṭār. avar kaṇṭa annikaḻccikkuriya iṭattiṉ amippum avarāl teḷivāka aṟiyappaṭṭatu. avviṭattiṉ kaṭ ceṅkuttāṉa periya veḷḷi malippāṟi yoṉṟu aṭiyāḷamāka niṟṟaliyum, avar kuṟippiṭṭār; aṅkē kāṇappaṭṭa makkaḷ āḻamāṉa paṉippāṟip piḷavukaḷiṉūṭē vaḷarntirunta maraṅkaḷiṉ uccikaḷit taṟittaluṅ kaṇṭār; am makkaḷiṉ uruva aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷiyum avarkaḷ aṇintirunta uṭikaḷiṉ kuṟikaḷiyuṅ kūṭac cevvaṉē terintu koṇṭār. ivvāṟu kaṇṭa kaṉaviṉiṉṟum avar viḻittavuṭaṉē, ak kaṉaviṉ teḷivum uṇmiyum avaratuḷḷatti uṟuttiṉa. tirumpavum avar ayarntu uṟaṅkat, tirumpavum akkaṉā nikaḻcci muṟkaṇṭapaṭiyē eḷḷaḷavum picakāmal appaṭiyē tōṉṟalāyiṟṟu.
viṭiyaṟ kāliyil avar paṭukkiyiṉiṉṟu eḻunta piṟakum ak kaṉaviṉ tōṟṟattit tam niṉiviṉiṉṟum akaṟṟal  muṭiyavilli. piṉṉarc ciṟitu nērattilellān tām etirppaṭṭa tamakkup paḻiya tōḻaṉāṉa oru vēṭṭuvaṉiṭan tāṅkaṇṭa ak kaṉā nikaḻcciyi eṭutturittār. atukēṭṭa avaṉ avar kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭa iṭan taṉakkun teriyu meṉṟu ciṟitum maliyātu viḷampiṉāṉ. viḷampiyapaṭiyē ammalip paḷḷattākkiṉ kaṇṇatāṉa avviṭattiyun terintu kāṭṭiṉāṉ. uṭaṉē at talivar makkaṭ kūṭṭam oṉṟu cērttuk, kōvēṟu kaḻutikaḷuṅ kampaḷikaḷum uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷun tokuttār.
kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭati nampi ivar ivvaḷavu ēṟpāṭu ceytal kaṇṭa aṇṭi ayalār ivari ēḷaṉañ ceytaṉar. ataṟkavar ciṟitum varuntātu yāṉ ituceyya vallamiyuṭiyēṉ; ākiyāl, itu ceyvēṉ; yāṉ kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭati uṇmi yeṉṟē nampukiṉṟēṉ eṉa moḻintu
viṭṭu, am malikaḷiṉ iṭiyē nūṟṟimpatu milkaḷukku appāl uḷḷa kārcaṉ paḷḷattākkuk kaṇavāykkut tām cērtta makkaṭ kūṭṭattāriyum piṟavaṟṟiyum pōkkiṉār. pōṉa ak kūṭṭattār. avar kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭapaṭiyē oru makkaṭkuḻu aṅkē peruntuyar uḻattalik kaṇṭu, avarkaḷuḷ iṟantu pōyiṉār oḻiya, eñciyavarkaḷi uyiruṭaṉ koṇarntaṉar.
iṉṉum, mūccu ōṭṭam niṉṟu uṇarviḻantu kiṭantār cilari iṟantupōṉavarākak karuti avar tam uṟaviṉar avarip putippatō erippatō ceytu viṭukiṉṟaṉar. ittakiya nikaḻccikaḷ ellā nāṭṭavarkaḷ iṭiyilum piḻiyāka nērntu viṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu orukāṟ cavappeṭṭiyil aṭakkañ ceyyappaṭṭa oruvar tamatu niṉiviṉ vaṉmiyāl tolivilirunta tam naṇparkkut tamatu iṭarppāṭāṉa nilimiyiṉik kaṉavilum naṉavilum aṟivittu avviṭariṉiṉṟum mīṇṭa meyvaralāṟṟiṉi iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭutum:
mika neruṅkiya naṭpuvāynta aṟiñar iruvaril oruvar oru kallūriyil āciriyarāy iruntaṉar. maṟṟoruvar avarkkup patiṉeṭṭuk kal tolivāṉatōriṭattil ōraluvalil amarntiruntaṉar. orunāṭ kallūriyāciriyar iravil ayarntu uṟaṅkukiyil, tam naṇpar tamatu kaṉaviṉkaṭ ṭōṉṟit tām uyiruṭaṉ putikkappaṭappōvatip peruntikiloṭu kūṟak kaṇṭār. kaṇṭu, ataṉāl neñcaṅ kalaṅki viḻitta avar tām kaṇṭatu kaṉavātali yuṇarntu āṟutaluṟṟu maṟupaṭiyun tūkkattiṟ ceṉṟār. maṟupaṭiyum ak kaṉavu appaṭiyē tōṉṟalāyiṟṟu. tirumpavum viḻittup pārkka, viṭintupōṉamiyāṟ kālik kaṭaṉkaḷi muṭittuc ciṟṟuṇṭi koṇṭu, avar oru kinnāṟkāliyil amarntiruntār. appōtu evarō oruvar katavit taṭṭum ōci kēṭṭatu. clnd mt®, ‘cŸns tUf! veṉṟu kūviṉār. aṭuttuk katavu tiṟakkum oliyum evarō uḷ nuḻiyum aravamun teṉpaṭṭaṉa. varupavaṉ vēlikkāraṉā yirukkalāmeṉak karuti avar nimirntu nōkkavilli, āṉāl, naṇparē, avarkaḷ eṉṉip putikkiṉṟārkaḷē! eṉat tam naṇpariṉ kural oli avar kātaṇṭik kucukucu veṉṟu nuḻintu avarit tiṭukkiṭac ceytatu. uṭaṉē avar eḻuntu pārkka avvaṟiyil evarun teṉpaṭṭilar; vēlik
kārarum evarum vantilar. ākavē, avar kālan tāḻātu puṟappaṭṭut tam naṇpar irukkum ūrukkuk kaṭitiṟ pōyiṉār. avaratu vīṭṭaṇṭic celkiyil, oru cavappeṭṭi ataṉ vāyililē appōtu tāṉ koṇarappaṭalāyiṟṟu. tam naṇpariṉ uṭampi ataṉuḷ vittu āṇiyaṟintu viṭṭup, putikku miṭattiṟku eṭuttup pōka aṅkiruntār puṟappaṭutalik kaṇṭār. kaṇṭatum, aṅkuḷḷāri nōkki ap peṭṭiyit tiṟakka vēṇṭumeṉṟuñ cavaccaṭaṅku ceyya lākāteṉṟum vaṟpuṟuttiṉār. aṅṅaṉamē peṭṭi tiṟakkappaṭṭatu; tam naṇpariṉ uṭampi ataṉiṉṟum eṭuppittup paṭukki mēṟ kiṭattic cūṭāṉa oṟṟaṭam ataṟkuk koṭuppittār. avvāṟu ceyyavē piṇamāyk kiṭanta avar uyirpeṟṟuc cila nāṭkaḷilellām muḻunala meytiṉār. ataṉpiṉṉar avar oṉpatāṇṭukaḷ uyir vāḻntār eṉpatu.
iḵtoṉṟō! oru pēriṭaril akappaṭṭa oruvaratu niṉivu orōvorukāl avaratu kaṉavu niliyiṟ pērāṟṟal vāyntatākit toliviṉkaṇ uḷḷa piṟarcilarkkut taṉṉi yuṭiyarātu uruvattit tōṟṟuvittu, avar akap paṭṭirukkum iṭarppāṭāṉa niliyiṉiyum aṟivittu, avari anniliyiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭa varalāṟukaḷum palappala; avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaṉi iṅkē varikuvam:
ilivarppūl eṉṉun tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉattiṟkum maṟṟoru tuṟimukap paṭṭiṉattiṟkum iṭiyilē ōṭik koṇṭirunta vāṇikakkappal oṉṟiṉ talivaṉum avaṉ tuṇivaṉum orunāṭ kappalil mēṟṟaṭṭil iruntavāṟē pakalavaṉ iyakkatti uṟṟu nōkkik kaṇakkuc ceytukoṇṭiruntaṉar; atu ceytapiṉ iruvarum aṟṟi nāḷ vēlik kaṇakkup pārttaṟkuk kīḻttaṭṭil iṟaṅkiṉar. iṟaṅkiya paṭikkaṭṭukku etirē yirunta catukkamāṉa oru veḷiyiṭattiṉ oru pakkattiṟ kappaṟṟalivaṉ irukkum oru ciṟṟaṟiyum, maṟṟoru pakkattil at talivaṉukkut tuṇivaṉirukkum aṟiyum iruntaṉa. tuṇivaṉirukkum aṟiyiṉ cāyvumēciyiṉ arukiruntu oruvaṉ taṉ ṟōḷvaḻiyē ciṟitu tirumpi nōkkiṉāl, talivaṉirukkum aṟiyiṉ uṭpakuti avaṉukku naṉṟāyt teriyum.
iṉit, taṉ aṟiyuḷiruntu kaṇakkuc ceytavaṉāṉa tuṇivaṉ ataṉi muṭittavuṭaṉ tirumpi, etir aṟiyiṉuḷ ḷirunta talivaṉi viḷittu, ‘nuṅkaḷ kaṇakku eppaṭi?  eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṉ. ataṟku viṭi varātu pōkavē, tirumpattirumpa ataṉiyē viṉavik kūviṉāṉ. aṅṅaṉaṅ kūvukiṉṟuḻit taṉ tōṭpakkamāyc ciṟitu tirumpi nōkka, ak kappaṟṟalivaṉ taṉatu kaṟpalakiyilē virivāy eḻutikkoṇṭiruppatu
pōl avaṟkuk kāṇappaṭṭāṉ. palakāṟ kūviyum ētum viṭivarāmi kaṇṭu, attuṇivaṉ veḷiyē pōntu talivaṉaṟiyiṉ katavaṇṭi niṉṟu uḷnōkkat, talivaṉ eṉṟu tāṉ piḻiyākak karutiya avvuruvan talinimirntu ivaṉip pārkka, aḵtoru putiya āṭavaṉiṉ uruvamākat tōṉṟak kaṇṭāṉ. kaṇṭa at tuṇivaṉ kōḻi neñcam uṭiyavaṉ allaṉ. eṉṟālum, ētum vāypēcātu avvuruvam ivaṉi uṟuttup pārkka, itaṟku muṉ tāṉ kaṇṭirāta av vāṭavaṉiṉ av uruvanili avaṟku accattiṉi viḷivi yātu oḻiyumō? ākavē, at tuṇivaṉ verukkoṇṭa neñciṉaṉāy, ataṉ mēl ētum viṉavātu, mēṟṟaṭṭiṉ mēl virintōṭik kappaṟ ṟalivaṉi aṇukiṉāṉ.
aṅṅaṉam veruṇṭu vantāṉi nōkki, naṇpaṉē, yātu niṉakku nērntatu? eṉṟu talivaṉ viṉava nuṅkaḷ cāyvumēciyaṇṭi uṭkārntiruppavar yār? eṉṟu avaṉ etir viṉāviṉāṉ. evarum illiyē! eṉṟu avaṉ urippa, mīṇṭum attuṇivaṉ yāṉ itaṟkumuṟ pārāta ōr āḷ aṅkē uṭkārntirukkiṉṟār! eṉṟu valiyuṟuttic coṉṉāṉ. ataṉ mēl iruvarum iṟumpūtuṟṟavarāy oruvari yoruvar maṟuttup pēcic, ciṟitu nērattiṟkup piṉ, iruvarumē oṉṟucērntu kīḻuḷḷa avvaṟiyaṇṭi ceṉṟaṉar. ataṉuḷ nōkka aṅkē evariyuṅ kaṇṭilar. ataṉmēṟ kappaṟṟalivaṉ taṉ tuṇivaṉi nōkki, nī kaṇṭatu kaṉavēyaṉṟip piṟi taṉṟeṉa muṉṉamē yāṉ colla villiyā? eṉṟaṉaṉ. aiya, colvatu eḷitu; yāṉ kaṇṭa āḷ meyyākavē nuṅkaḷatu kaṟpalakimēl eḻutiyati nāṉ pārttirāviṭṭāl, maṟupaṭiyum yāṉ eṉ illattiyum maṉivi makkaḷiyuṅ kāṇā toḻivēṉāka! eṉṟu tuṇivaṉ mīṇṭum uṟuttik kūṟiṉaṉ. atu kēṭṭa talivaṉ aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ akkaṟpalakimēl av āḷ eḻutiya eḻuttu irukka vēṇṭumaṉṟō? eṉa moḻintu, ataṉi yeṭuttuk kāṇak, kappali vaṭamēṟkil ōṭṭuka! eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭar ataṉkaṇ eḻutappaṭṭiruntatu.
ataṉmēl attalivaṉ peritun tikippuṟṟavaṉāy ataṉi ārāyvāṉ pukuntu taṉ tuṇivaṉiyum, ammarak
kalattiluḷḷār aṉivariyum aḻittu, ak kaṟpalakiyil variyappaṭṭirunta accoṟ ṟoṭariṉ kīḻ, ataṉip pōlavē variyumpaṭi ovvoruvariyum ēviṉāṉ; aṅṅaṉamē varintavaṟṟil oruvaratu ki yeḻuttāvatu ataṉōṭu ottatāy irukkavilli. ataṉmēl atil ētāvatu ōr uṇmiyirukka vēṇṭumeṉṟu ellārumē oru muṭivukku vara, ak kappaṟṟalivaṉ taṉatu kaṟpalakiyil variyappaṭṭa accoṟṟoṭariṉ paṭiyē taṉ kappali vaṭamēṟku mukamāy ōṭṭu vippāṉāyiṉaṉ.
kappal ammukamāy ōṭukiyiṟ, piṟpakal mūṉṟu maṇikkup, perum paṉippāṟi oṉṟum ataṉ aṇṭiyil oru marakkalamun teṉpaṭalāyiṉa. uṭaṉē, talivaṉ taṉ marakkalatti avviraṇṭaṟkuñ ciṟitu tolivilēyē niṟuttit, taṉatu tolivu nōkkik kaṇṇāṭiyiṉāl nōkka, oru kappal paṉippāṟikaḷiṉ naṭuvē akappaṭṭu uṟintu pōkavum, ataṉkaṇ makkaḷ palar tuṉpuṟṟanili
yilirukkavuṅ kaṇṭu, uṭaṉē taṉatu marakkalattiluḷḷa paṭakukaḷ cilavaṟṟik kīḻ iṟakkuvittup pōkkap, paṉippāṟiyil tākkuṇṭa marakkalatti aviyaṇuki, atilirunta makkaḷ aṉivariyuṅ koṇarvittaṉa.
mūṉṟāvatu, paṭaku koṇarnta makkaḷ immarakkalattiṉ mēl ēṟukiyil, avarkaḷuḷ oruvaṉatu mukatti mēliruntu kaṇṭa tuṇivaṉ peruntikil koṇṭāṉ. ēṉeṉil, avaṉ taṉ kappaṟṟalivaṉatu aṟiyil mūṉṟallatu nāṉkumaṇi nērattiṟkumuṟ kaṇṭatu aṉṉavaṉatu mukamēyākum. mutalil, ivaṉ tāṉ kaṇṭatu maṉattiṉ poyyāṉa tōṟṟa māyirukkalāmō veṉa eṇṇiṉāṉ. āṉāl, av āḷiṉ mukamē yaṉṟi uruva aṭiyāḷamum avaṉ aṇintirunta uṭiyum muṉṉē tāṉ kaṇṭa uruvattiṉ aṭiyāḷattiyum uṭiyiyum muḻutum ottiruntaṉa. mīṭkappaṭṭa vaḻippōkkarkaḷuṅ kappal vēli yāṭkaḷum paṭṭiṉiyum paciyumāyk kiṭantu vantavarkaḷāta lāl, avarkaṭku vēṇṭum vacatikaḷellām uṭaṉē ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. piṉṉark, kappaṟṟalivaṉun taṉatu kappali ōṭṭuvāṉāyiṉāṉ. aṅṅaṉam aḵtu ōṭukiyil, at tuṇivaṉ talivaṉpāṟ pōntu avaṉi orupuṟan taṉiyē yaḻittu, aiya, yāṉ iṟṟip pakaliṟ kaṇṭatu pēy uruvam aṉṟu. yāṉ kaṇṭa āḷ atō! nammāl mīṭkappaṭṭa kuḻuviṉaruḷ oruvarāy irukkiṉṟār! yāṉ nuṅkaḷatu kaṟpalakimēl eḻutakkaṇṭa āḷ avarēyeṉa āṇi yiṭṭuk kūṟuvēṉ! eṉṟāḷ. atukēṭṭa talivaṉ, paṉip pāṟiyiṉ naṭuvē akappaṭṭuk koṇṭa marakkalattilirunta āḷ, vēṟu tolivil ōṭik koṇṭirunta namatu kappalukku evvāṟu caṭutiyil vantu  caṭutiyil maṟiyak kūṭum? iḵtu ētō oru perummāyamā yirukkiṉṟatu! itaṉi ārāyntu pārppōm, vā! eṉṟu kūṟi, avaṉōṭu am mīṭkappaṭṭa kuḻuviṉariṭañ ceṉṟāṉ.
appōtu am māya āḷ tāṉ ēṟiyirunta kappaliṉ mīkāmaṉiṭam pēcik koṇṭiruntāṉ. ivviruvarun tampāl varakkaṇṭa avviruvarum uṭaṉē etirpōntu, tammi akkoṭun tuṉpattiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭa attalivaṟkun tuṇivaṟkum neñcāra naṉṟimoḻi colli vāḻttiṉar. tām attakiya perun tuṉpattil akappaṭṭirukkak kāṇiṉ avarkaḷun tammi aṅṅaṉamē mīṭpatu uṟutiyātalāl tāñ ceytatu kaṭamiyē yeṉa moḻintu, avviruriyum taṉ aṟikku at talivaṉ aḻittuc ceṉṟāṉ; ceṉṟatum am mīkāmaṉuṭaṉ vanta vaḻippōkkaṉi nōkki aiya! yāṉ, ētō kuṟumpu paṇṇukiṉṟēṉ eṉṟu niṉiyāmal, intak kaṟpalaki mēṟ kappali vaṭa mēṟkil oṭṭuka! eṉṉuñ coṟṟoṭari eḻutuṅkaḷ eṉṟu talivaṉ vēṇṭiṉāṉ. ittuṇip pērutaviceyta tāṅkaḷ etu vēṇṭumāyiṉuñ ceyvēṉ eṉap pukaṉṟu, ak kaṟpalakimēl avvāṟē av vaḻip pōkkaṉum varintāṉ. varinta piṉ talivaṉ ak kaṟpalakiyi vāṅki maṟivāyp puṟantiruppi itu nuṅkaḷ ki yeḻuttuttāṉā? eṉṟu pāruṅkaḷ! eṉak kēṭṭāṉ. ataṟkavaṉ ippōtu tāṉ ataṉi eḻutiṉēṉē atippaṟṟi aiyam ēṉ! eṉak kūṟiṉāṉ. piṟaku talivaṉ avaṉ etirilēyē ak kaṟpalakiyip puṟantiruppik kāṭṭa, ataṉkaṇ muṉṉum piṉṉum atē coṟṟoṭar atē kiyeḻuttil eḻutappaṭṭiruttalip pārttu, avaṉ aiya, yāṉ orumuṟi tāṉē itaṉkaṇ eḻutiṉēṉ. maṟṟoru muṟiyum itaṉi eḻutiṉavar yāvar? eṉa viṉāyiṉāṉ. ataṟkut talivaṉ yāṉ aṟiyēṉ. iṟṟippakal nīr tām intac cāyvu mēciyaṇṭiyiruntu eḻutiṉīr eṉa eṉtuṇivaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ. eṉṟaṉaṉ; atukēṭṭa avvaḻip pōkkaṉum avaṉ ēṟivanta marakkala mīkāmaṉum oruvari yoruvar nōkki iṟumpūtuṟṟaṉar piṟaku, ammarakkalamīkāmaṉ akkappaṟṟalivaṉi nōkki iṟṟik kāliyil iv vaḻippōkkar kaḷippiṉāl ayarntu uṟaṅkiṉār. viḻittapiṉ ivar eṉṉi nōkki nammi ip pēriṭariṉiṉṟu mīṭpataṟku oru kappal varukiṉṟatu; ak kappaliṉ mēṟṟaṭṭiṉmēl yāṉ pōyiruntatākavuṅ kaṉavu kaṇṭēṉ. eṉṟu kūṟi, ak kappaliṉ aṭiyāḷattiyum viritturit tār. avvaṭiyāḷappaṭiyē intak kappaliṟ pōntu nīṅkaḷum eṅkaḷi mīṭṭuk kāppāṟṟiṉīrkaḷ! eṉa moḻintaṉaṉ āṉāl, ak kaṉaviṉkaṇ tāṉ kaṟpalakimēl ētum eḻuti ṉatākat taṉakku niṉippilli yeṉṟum, eṉṟālum ikkappaliṉ mēṟṟaṭṭiṉ mēluḷḷa ovvoṉṟum yāṉ aṟintavikaḷākavē kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, ik kappalil ēṟi yaṟiyāta eṉakku iṅkuḷḷavikaḷ ellām naṉku terintavikaḷākak kāṇappaṭuvatu periyatoru putumiyāy irukkiṉṟateṉṟum av vaḻippōkkaṉ viyappuṭaṉ moḻintaṉaṉ.
uṇmiyāka nikaḻnta innikaḻcciyil vaḻippōkkaṉ kaṇṭa kaṉaviṉ nilitāṉ peritum viyakkat takkatāyirukkiṉṟatu. paṉip pāṟikaḷiṉūṭē yakappaṭṭuk koṇṭa marakkalattiṟ paṭṭiṉiyum paciyumāyk kiṭanta av vaḻippōkkaṉatu niṉivu, avaṉatu kaṉaviṉkaṇ mikka curucuruppu uṭiyatāyt taṟpātukāppiṉ poruṭṭu, vēṟoru marakkalattiṉkaṇ avaṉatu nuṇṇuṭampiṉ uruviṉit tōṟṟuvittal, tāṉirukkum marakkalattiṉ mukamāy ammarakkalam varumāṟuṅ kaṟpala kiyil eḻutuvittut taṉṉiyun taṉṉuṭaṉ iruntā riyum oruṅku uyirpiḻikkac ceytatu. kaṉaviṉ kaṭ kaṇṭa vikaḷip perumpālum naṉaviṉkaṇ maṟantu pōtal ellār māṭṭuṅ kāṇappaṭutaliṉ, av vaḻip pōkkaṉ taṉ kaṉaviṉkaṇ nuṇṇuṭampiliruntu kaṟpalakiyil eḻutiyati maṟantu pōyiṉa ṉākaliṉ, atu paṟṟi īṇṭik kāvatōr iḻukkilli. iṅṅaṉamākac ciṟcilarkku avartam niṉivu kaṉaviṉkaṭ ciṟcila nēraṅkaḷil mikka āṟṟaluṭiyatāy iyaṅkum uṇmiyum, aḵtu akkālaṅkaḷil neṭun toli viluḷḷārkkun taṉṉiyuṭiyāṉatu nuṇ uruviṉit tōṟṟuvittuc ceyaṟkariya ceyyun taṉmiyuṅ karuttiṟ patikkaṟpālaṉa vākumeṉka.
iṅṅaṉamākak kaṉaviṭic curucuruppāy iyaṅkum oruvaṉatu niṉivu avaṉatu nuṇṇuruviṉip piṟa ṉoruvaṉukku naṉaviṉkaṭ ṭōṟṟuvittal pōlavē, naṉaviṉ kaṇ oruvaṉ uṟuttu niṉinta niṉivum avaṉatu nuṇṇuruviṉik kaṉaviṉkaṇ uḷḷār cilarkkut tōṟṟuvittalum uṇṭu. ivvuṇmiyiṉi viḷakkuñ cila uṇmi nikaḻccikaḷi iṅṅaṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām:
ceṉṟa maḻikālattil oru ñāyiṟṟukkiḻami yiravu oru maṇikku, eṉatu vīṭṭukku mūṉṟukal toliviluḷḷatoru vīṭṭilirunta eṉ nēyar iruvaratu kaṉaviṉ kaṇ eṉatu uruviṉit tōṟṟuvikka virumpi uṟuttu niṉintēṉ. piṉṉarc cila nāṭceṉṟu yāṉ avarkaḷip pārttapōtu yāṉ ceyta av vārāycciyiṉip paṟṟi vēṇṭumeṉṟē ētuñ collātiruntēṉ. āṉāl, avarkaḷ eṉṉōṭu uriyāṭikkoṇṭu varukiyil oruvar eṉṉi nōkkic, ceṉṟa ñāyiṟṟukkiḻami evvaḷavu putumiyāṉa ōr iraviṉik kaḻittōm eṉṟu coṉṉāl nīr nampamāṭṭīr! eṉṟu mutaliṟ collip piṉṉar eṉatu uruvan tamatu aṟiyil niṟkak kaṇṭa kāṭciyiṉi viritturittār. eṉatu uruvattōṟṟam mikavum teḷivāy iruntamiyāl, avarkaḷ viḻitteḻuntu tamatu nāḻiki vaṭṭili nōkka appōtu maṇi oṉṟāyirukkak kaṇṭārkaḷ, eṉpatu. innikaḻccik kaṇniṉintavaratu niṉivu orumukamāy uṟittu niṉṟamiyāl, anniṉiviṉ uṟippukkuttaka,  avaratu nuṇṇuruvu avar tam nēyaratu kaṉaviṉkaṇ mikavun teḷivuṭiya tāyt tōṉṟa lāyiṟṟu.
iṉṉum, nāṭṭuppuṟattu mātāṉa perttā eṉpavaḷ pāricumānakarattu maruttuva viṭutiyiṟ kuḻanti maruttuvañ ceyyum vēliyil vantu amarntapōtu, avviṭutit talimi maruttuvarkku aṟimukam āyiṉaḷ. ammātu eḷitilē aṟituyiliṟ celuttappaṭum iyaṟki vāyntavaḷ avaḷatu avviyaṟki aṟinta av viṭutit talivar tām tolivi liruntapaṭiyē avaḷip palakāl aṟituyiliṟ celuttiyirukkiṉṟār. iṅṅaṉam ammātu eḷitilē aṟituyiliṟ celvatu maṭṭum aṉṟu, toliviluḷḷāriyun tāṉ niṉinta paṭi naṭatta valla niṉivāṟṟalun taṉakkuṇṭeṉa moḻivaḷ. ivvāṟṟāl taṉakkut taṉ aṉṉiyiṭa miruntu piṟappurimiyāka vantateṉpatum avaḷatu karuttu. tāṉ eṇṇiṉāl naṭappavar taṭukkiviḻavum vaḻipicakippōkavun, kuṟippiṭṭa iṭattiṟkup pōkāmal taṭippaṭavuñ ceytal kūṭum eṉpaḷ. iṅṅaṉamellāñ ceyyakkūṭiya taṉ niṉivāṟṟali avaḷ avviṭutiyiṉ maruttuvat talivarkkup palakāṟ pulappaṭuttik kāṭṭa, avarum ataṉi oppuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟār. avaḷ attalivarkkē tāṉ eṇṇiya toru tōṟṟattiṉi avar kaṭpulaṉetirē viḷivikka avar ataṉippaṟṟi varintatu varumāṟu:
yāṉ naṉṟāka uṟaṅkum iyalpiṉēṉ. eṉ uṟakkattiṉ iṭiyē eppōtāyiṉum yāṉ viḻittatāka eṉakku niṉivilli āṉāl, orunāḷiravu iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu maṇikkuc caṭutiyil yāṉ viḻitteḻuntēṉ. eṉ kaṇkaḷit tiṟavāmalē itu perttāviṉ cūḻalāyirukkalām eṉa niṉintēṉ ataṉpiṟ kaṇkaḷit tiṟantu eṉakketirēyirunta cuvari nōkka, ataṉkaṇ vaṭṭamāṉa ōr oḷivaṭi
viṉik kaṇṭēṉ; aḵtu oru kommaṭṭippaḻa aḷaviṉatāyk kāṇap paṭṭatu; ciṟitu nēram yāṉ ataṉi uṟṟunōkkiya
paṭiyāy iruntēṉ; piṉṉar atu maṟintupōyiṟṟu. tirumpavum nāṉ naṉṟāyp paṭuttut tūṅkiviṭṭēṉ. maṟunāṭkaliyil perttā eṉṉip pārttapōtu, avaḷ mutalil eṉ paṭukkiyic cūḻac cila cila nāykaḷ tōṉṟumāṟum, iraṇṭāvatu cilar caṇṭiyiṭumāṟum, mūṉṟāvatu oru viḷakku veḷiccaṅ kāṇappaṭumāṟun tāṉ uṟuttu niṉintatākap pukaṉṟaṉaḷ. mutal iraṇṭum eṉakkut tōṉṟilavāyiṉum, mūṉṟāvatu avaḷ eṇṇiyapaṭiyē oru viḷakkiṉoḷi eṉakkut tōṉṟiyatu. eṉpatu.
piṉṉar orukāl ammaruttuvattalivarum avar tam naṇpar oruvarumāka oru vaṇṭiyil ōriṭattiruntu piṟi tōriṭattiṟkup pōykkoṇṭirukkiyil, avarkaḷ ceṉṟa nēram naḷḷiravātalāl teru veṅkum viḷakkēṟṟappaṭṭu veḷiccamuṭiyatāyiruntum, añcattakka kāraṇam ētoṉṟumē illātiruntum, ammaruttuvarukkut tiṭīreṉa uḷḷattil ōr accantōṉṟiyatu. ō! itu perttāviṉ cūḻalāyirukkalā meṉṟu avar  colkiyil, avartam naṇparum nakittuk koṇṭu, ā! eṉṉi yaṟiyāmalē eṉakkum uḷḷattē oru tikil tōṉṟukiṉṟa teṉṟār. it tikilāṉatu kuṟippiṭṭa iṭattiṟkuppōyc cērum variyil avviruvarukkum iruntatu. piṉṉar avarkaḷ vaṇṭiyi viṭṭuk kīḻ iṟaṅkukiyil, avartam naṇpar vāṉattil eviyō veṇmiyāṉavi tam kaṇṇetirē paṟantu cellak kaṇṭār. āṉāl, ataṉi ammaruttuvat talivar kāṇavilli.
aṭuttanāḷ attalivar tamatu maruttuva viṭutiyiṟ perttāvip pārttapōtu nēṟṟu iravu tāmun tam naṇparum aṭinta nilimikaḷic collumpaṭi avaḷi vēṇṭa, avaḷuñ colvāḷāyiṉaḷ; mutalil nuṅkaḷ vaṇṭikkāraṉ vaṇṭiyi vaḻitappip pala mukamāy ōṭṭikkoṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ; āṉāl, vaṇṭikkuḷḷirunta nīṅkaḷ ataṉi uṇaravilli eṉṟaṉaḷ. ivaḷ itu colliyapiṟakutāṉ tamatu vaṇṭi vaḻitappip palamukamāy ōṭṭappaṭṭami avaratu niṉivukku vantatu. ataṉ piṉ avaḷ, tuvakkattil nīṅkaḷum ataṉpiṉ nuṅkaḷ naṇparum ētoru kāraṇamum iṉṟiyē peruntikil koṇṭīrkaḷ! ataṉ piṉṉar veḷḷiya puṟākkaḷ cila nuṅkaḷic cuṟṟi arukilē paṟantu cellak kaṇṭīrkaḷ! eṉa moḻintaṉaḷ.
pāruṅkaḷ! viḻippil niṉivuṭaṉ iyaṅkumpōtē perttā eṉpavaḷ tāṉ uṟuttu niṉinta niṉivukaḷ, viḻippil iyaṅkiya ammaruttuvat talivarkkum avartam naṇparkkum avar tam vaṇṭikkāraṟkum akattēyum puṟattēyum tōṉṟi avartam uḷḷaṅkaḷit tamvaḻip paṭuttiviṭṭaṉa.  yām mika iḷiyamāyiruntapōtē emmik kāṇavanta oruvar, em etirilēyē oru kaṟpalakiyi yeṭuttu atil ētō oṉṟu varintapiṉ ataṉi appāl maṟittuvittu, ētēṉum oru pūvi niṉittuc collumpaṭi emmik kēṭṭār; aṅṅaṉamē oru pūviṉ peyari yām colla (coṉṉa appūviṉ peyar ippōtu niṉivil illi), uṭaṉē avar tāmeḻutiya akkaṟpalakiyi yeṭuttukkāṭṭa, ataṉkaṇ muṉṉamē appūviṉ peyar avarāl varintu vikkappaṭṭiruttal kaṇṭu viyappuṟṟēm. iṅṅaṉamē avar toṭarpākap pala poruṭ peyarkaḷi muṉṉeḻutip piṉ ovvoṉṟāka emmi niṉittuc collumpaṭi kēṭka, yām coṉṉaviyellām picakāmal muṉṉamē avarāl varintu vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
iṅṅaṉamāka naṉavilēyē oruvar uṟuttu niṉitta niṉiviṉpaṭiyē niṉikkappaṭṭavarum naṉaviṉkaṇ niṉittalum, uṟuttu niṉinta poruḷkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ niṉikkappaṭṭavariṉ kaṭpulaṉetirē naṉaviṉkaṇ viḷaṅkit tōṉṟutalum āṅkāṅku uṇmiyāka nikaḻntu varutali eṇṇippārkkuṅkāl, mantirakārariṟ cilar tamakkup pakivarāyiṉār vēṟu cilarkku ēval pilli vittu avarit tuṉpuṟuttuvatellām perumpālum avar tam niṉi vāṟṟaliṉālēyē viḷivikkappaṭukiṉṟa teṉṉum uṇmi naṉku viḷaṅkāniṟkum. ammantirakārar pēykaḷi ēvi viṭṭu avvāṟu ceyvikkiṉṟaṉareṉpatu muṟṟumē uṇmiyaṉṟu. avar kaṉaviṉum naṉaviṉum uṟuttu niṉinta tīya niṉivu kaḷiṉ tīya vaṭivaṅkaḷē, avarkkup pakiyāyiṉārkkup pēy vaṭivaṅkaḷākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mantirakārar tām tuṉpuṟuttutaṟku muṉinta piṟar ammantirakārariṉum āṟṟal mikka maṉavorumi yuṭiyarāy, avar ēviya avvaṭivaṅ kaḷit tirumpiccellumāṟu tāmum uṟuttu niṉipparāyiṉ, at tīya vaṭivaṅkaḷ avari varuttāvāyt tirumpiccellum. iṅṅaṉan tamakkup pakiyāṉa mantirakārar cilar tamakkut tīṅkiḻittali emmiṭañcolli muṟiyiṭṭa aṉpar cilarkku, yām ivvivvāṟu ceyka! eṉṟu kaṟpittē māka, avar avvāṟē ceyya, uṭaṉē attīyavaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ tīya ceyal oḻintu pōyateṉṟu collak kēṭṭēm. ivviyalpukaḷi maṉitavaciyam eṉṉum ematu nūliṉkaṇ virittu viḷakkiyirukkiṉṟēmākaliṉ, mīṇṭum avikaḷi iṅṅaṉamākap pēriṭaril akappaṭṭāratu niṉivu avaratu kaṉaviṉkaṇ mikka curucuruppuṭiyatāy iyaṅkik kaṉavilum naṉavilum uḷḷārkkut taṉṉi yuṭiyāratu nilimiyiṉi aṟivittal pōlavē naṉavilē uṟuttu niṉippāratu niṉivuṅkaṉavilum naṉavilum uḷḷār piṟarkkun taṉṉip pulaṉākkutal kāṇka.
iṉṉum, aṉpuṭiyār iruvaril oruvarkku nērum iṭam uṭaṉē maṟṟavarkkuṅ kaṉaviṉkaṭ ṭōṉṟutalik kāṭṭum iraṇṭu uṇmi varalāṟukaḷi īṇṭeṭuttuk kāṭṭutum:
eṉ tantiyārkkum eṉakkum mikutiyāṉa aṉpu uṇṭu. oru nāṭ pakal kaṭumiyāṉa vēlikaḷic ceytu viṭṭu iravu eṭṭu maṇikku vīṭṭukkup pōy, uṭaṉē uṇavukoṇṭu, kaḷippiṉāl uṭaṉē uṟaṅkac ceṉṟēṉ. eṉtali taliyaṇimēṟpaṭṭatum mika ayarnta tuyiliṟ ceṉṟēṉ: eṉ maṉivi paṭukki yaṇṭi vantatukūṭa eṉakkut teriyātu. avvāḻnta tuyiliṉ kaṇ eṉ tantiyār oru paṭikkaṭṭiṉ uyarattiliruntu vaḻukkik kīḻē viḻak kaṇṭēṉ. kaṇṭatum, avari viḻāmaṟ piṭittuk koḷvataṟkāka eṉpaṭukkik kālaṇṭi patittuk kutittēṉ. aṅṅaṉaṅ kutittatāṟ periyatōr ōci uṇṭāyiṟṟu. eṉ maṉivi uṭaṉē viḻittukkoṇṭu yātu kuṟumpu ceykiṟīr eṉṟu viṉāviṉāḷ. yāṉ viḷakkēṟṟi eṉatu kik kaṭikāratti nōkkiṉēṉ. maṇi iraṇṭēkāl āyiṟṟu. yāṉ kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭati avaṭku eṭuttu moḻintēṉ. avaḷ ataṉi nakiyāṭi viṭumpaṭi eṉṉit tūṇṭiṉāḷ; āṉāl, atu kūṭavilli. ataṉpiṉ avviravil yāṉ tūṅkavēyilli. eṉ tantiyār kīḻviḻuntu kāyam aṭintirukka vēṇṭumeṉṉum eṇṇam aḻuttamāka eṉṉuḷḷattiṟ patintu viṭṭamiyāl, yāṉ tirumpap paṭukki yaṇṭi cellāmal, eppōtu viṭiyum, viṭiyum eṉṟu patippuṭaṉ kāttiruntēṉ. pakalavaṉ kiḻakkē eḻak kaṇṭatum, yāṉ paṭṭiṉattiṟkup pōyk kampic ceyti viṭuttu, avaratu nalattippaṟṟik kēṭṭēṉ. ataṟku yāṉ peṟṟa viṭiyil, eṉ tantiyār paṭikkaṭṭiṉ mēliruntu viḻuntu kaṭuṅkāyam aṭinta nikaḻcci, yāṉ kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭavāṟē atēnērattil nērntami kuṟikkappaṭṭiruttal kaṇṭu uḷan tuṭittēṉ. eṉakku neṭuntolivilirunta eṉ tantiyār kīḻ viḻuntami eṉakkut tōṉṟalāyatu eṅṅaṉameṉṟu eṇṇippārttum yāṉ ataṉi yaṟiyakkūṭa villi yeṉṟu akkaṉavukaṇṭa turimakaṉ vāṉa nūlāciriyar oruvarkku eḻutiyirukkiṉṟār.
iṉṉum, orukāl vēṟoru turimakaṉ tāṉ pārttu vanta aluvali orunoṭi nērattil viṭṭu vilakavēṇṭumeṉṟu aṟikki vantatu. appōtu avar tammaṉiviyār avar irunta iṭattiṟku aṟupatu kaltoliviluḷḷatoru nāṭṭup puṟattil taṅkiyiruntār. atturimakaṉār tām iṅkilāntiṟkup pōm vaḻiyiṟpērūtu eṉṉum ūrkkum pōka vēṇṭiyavarāṉār. ātalāl avar tām pōkum avviṭattiṟku uṭaṉē tam piṉṉē vantucērumpaṭi tam maṉiviyārkku oru kaṭitam varintu pōkkiviṭṭut, tām avvūrkkuc ceṉṟār.
ītiṅṅaṉamirukka, nāṭṭup puṟattilirunta avartam maṉiviyār tamkaṇavar tamakkuk kaṭitam eḻutiya atē nāḷ iravil vaḻakkam pōṟ paṭukkikkuc ceṉṟār. ceṉṟu āḻnta tūkkattil irukkiyil, evarō tammip piṭittiḻukkak kaṉavukaṇṭu viḻitteḻuntu paṭukkimēl uṭkārntaṉar. appōtu avvammiyāratu ceviyiṉuḷ nī ēṉ aṅkē paṭuttirukkiṉṟāy? niṉ kaṇavaṉ niṉṉi varavēṇṭu kiṉṟāṉ; eḻuntu avaṉpāṟ cel! eṉṉum oli kucukucu veṉṟu nuḻintatu. āyiṉum, anta ammiyār maṟupaṭiyum paṭuttu uṟaṅka muyaṉṟār; āṉāl, tirumpattirumpa atē oli mūṉṟu muṟi tamatu kātiṉuḷ vantolikkak kēṭṭu, avar peritum accaṅkoḷḷa, ataṉāl avaratu neṟṟiyil viyarvi peruntuḷikaḷākat tōṉṟalāyiṉa, appōtu avaratu aṇṭiyiṟ paṭuttirunta ēvaṟkāri tuyil kalintu ammā! nīṅkaḷ naṭantu koṇṭum pēcik koṇṭumā irukkiṉṟīrkaḷ? eṉaviṉaviṉāḷ. ataṟku illi, vēṟu yārō appaṭic ceykiṟār, nīyā? eṉṟu avvammiyār etir viṉaviṉār. illiyammā, yāṉ paṭukkiyi viṭṭu aciyavilli;  āṉāl, nīṅkaḷ evaruṭaṉō pēcukiṉṟīr kaḷē eṉṟu ēvaṟkāri moḻintāḷ. accoṟkēṭṭapiṉ, itil ētō uṇmiyirukkavēṇṭu meṉṉum nampikki varappeṟṟavarāki anta ammi paṭukkiyi viṭṭuk kīḻē kutittut, tamatu kutirikkuc cēṇam iṭṭuk koṇarac coṉṉār. avvīṭṭiliruntavar ovvoruvarum av vammikku mūḷi picakip pōṉateṉṟu karintu, avari maṟupaṭiyum tūṅkavikka muyaṉṟaṉar. āṉāl, avvammi ataṟku aṭaṅkārāyk kutiri mītu ivarntu, pāṟikaḷiṉ mītuñ catuppu nilaṅkaḷiṉ ūṭum ataṉi virivāy ūrntu vantu vaṇṭippēṭṭiyic cērntār. appōtutāṉ oru nalla kutirivaṇṭi puṟappaṭat toṭaṅkiyatu. kaṭavuḷum avar pakkamiruntār. viyarkka viṟuviṟukkac cēṟum puḻutiyum paṭinta uṭiyuṭaṉ avvammi varutalik kaṇṭa avvaṇṭik kāraṉ, avar kikkuṟi kāṭṭiyatik kaṇṭu vaṇṭiyi niṟutti avvammiyi ataṉuḷḷē ēṟṟikkoṇṭu ataṉik kaṭuka ōṭṭicceṉṟāṉ. ataṉāl avvammiyār tam kaṇavaṉ ēṟiccella vēṇṭiya kappal pērūtu nakari viṭṭup puṟappaṭutaṟku oru nāḷ muṉṉāṭiyē avarpāṟ pōyc cērntaṉar. aṅṅaṉañ cērntamiyiṉālētāṉ, taṅ kaṇavaṉuṭiya ēṟpāṭukaḷi, iṅkilāntukkup puṟappaṭum muṉṉarē, kalantupēci oḻuṅkuceyyak kūṭiyavarāṉār.
pārmiṉkaḷ! innikaḻcciyiṟ pōnta turimakaṉār tamakkut tiṭīreṉa vanta kaṭṭaḷiyāl tām irukkumiṭattic caṭutiyil viṭṭu nīṅkumāṟu nērnta kaṭṭāyattiyun, tammaṉiviyār tammuṭaṉ iṉṟi neṭuntolivil appōtu iruntamiyāl avar eppaṭi uṭaṉaḻittuc celvateṉpa tiyum niṉintu maṉam varuntit tam maṉivikkuk kaṭitam eḻutiyakkāl, annērattil orumukappaṭṭu niṉṟa avaratu uṇarvāṉatu tolivilirunta avar tam maṉiviyārkkuk kaṉavilum naṉavilun tōṉṟi avvammiyārit taṅ kaṇavaṉpāl uṭaṉē puṟappaṭṭu varumpaṭi ceytatu.
iṉi, muṉṉorukāl aṉpiṉāl uruki oṉṟupaṭṭa nēya uḷḷam uṭiyarāṉa naṇpar iruvar, piṉṉoru kāl nērnta piḻiyāl oruvari yoruvar veṟuttup pirintu pōtali āṅkāṅkuk kāṇkiṉṟōm. aṅṅaṉam avar pirintu pōyiṉum, avariruvaruḷḷamun tūyavāyiruppiṉ, avaratu paḻiya nēya uṇarcci avviruvarkkuṅ kaṉaviṉkaṭ ṭōṉṟi, avari mīṇṭum oruṅkukūṭṭi aḷavaḷāvac ceytalum orōvaḻi nikaḻā niṟkiṉṟatu. itaṉi meyppaṭuttum ōr uṇmi varalāṟṟiṉi īṇṭu eṭuttuk kāṭṭutum. kiṟittuvarulaku eṉṉum veḷiyīṭṭukku oruvar eḻutiyatu varumāṟu:
nāṟpatu āṇṭukaḷāka eṉakku naṇparāyirukkum oruvar amerikka tēyattil irukkiṉṟār. patiṉāṉku āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ nērnta oru picakāl emakkuḷ maṉavēṟṟumiyuṇṭākak kaṭitappōkku varavu aṭiyoṭu niṉṟu pōyiṉa. aṅṅaṉamirukka 1898 ām āṇṭu navamparttiṅkaḷil oru nāḷiravu mikavum teḷivāṉa oru kaṉāk kaṇṭēṉ. eṉakku aiyāyiramil tolivāṉatōr iṭattilirunta eṉ paḻiya nēyarippaṟṟi appōtu yāṉ niṉivukūrtaṟku ētoru kāraṇamum illātiruntum, avar paḻamipōl eṉakketirē vantuniṉṟu, ētō muṉṉuṇṭāṉa oru picakāl tamakkuḷ nērnta piriviṉikkākat tām peruntuyar kūrvatāṟ, pōṉatu pōka iṉimēl nām muṉpōl aṉpāyiruttalē ceyaṟpālateṉa āṟṟāmiyōṭu moḻiyak kēṭṭu, avarum yāṉum mīṇṭum orumittu aḷavaḷāvuvatāka eṉatu kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭēṉ. kaṇṭu viḻittuppārkka aḵtoru veṟuṅkaṉavātal terintu ēmāntēṉ.
āṉāl, iraṇṭu kiḻamikaḷ kaḻittu, atē naṇpariṭa
miruntu eṉakku vanta kaṭitam periyatōr iṟumpūtiṉi viḷivittatu. akkaṭitattil avar, yāṉ nummippaṟṟi oru viyappāṉa kaṉavukaṇṭēṉ. patiṉāṉku āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ nīr eṉṉiṭam evvaḷavu aṉpuṭaṉ aḷavaḷāviṉīrō avvaḷavu aṉpuṭaṉ aḷavaḷāvip, pōṉatupōka veṉṟu atiṟ kūṟakkaṇṭēṉ. ataṉ karuttu iṉṉateṉa aṟintilēṉāyiṉum, nummik kāṇumporuṭṭē iṅkilāntiṟkup puṟappaṭu
kiṉṟēṉ. yāṉ kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭapaṭiyē niṟivēṟumeṉa nampukiṉṟēṉ eṉavarintiruntaṉar. ataṉpaṭiyē avar eṉpāl vara, avarum yāṉum ematu kaṉavip paṟṟi oruvarkkoruvar collik koṇṭōm. yāṅkaḷ kuṟittuvittirunta kuṟippukaḷi ottiṭṭup pārkkiyil, akkaṉākkaḷ iraṇṭum orē taṉmiyavāymaṭṭum iṉṟi orē nērattil iruvarukkun tōṉṟiṉavikaḷākavum iruttal aṟintēm eṉpatu.
iṉit, tīrāk koṭunōy koṇṭu varuntuvār cilarkku atu tīrkkum maruntu kaṉaviṉkaṇ aṟivikkap paṭutalum, āṅkāṅku nikaḻāniṟkiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam nikaḻum aṟivippu, nōy koṇṭāriṉ aṟivu kaṉavuṭampiṉkaṇ orōvaḻi mācu nīṅki viḷaṅkutalāl atu taṉṉiṉiṉṟē pulaṉākiṉṟatō, aṉṟi āvi vaṭiviṉ kaṇ ulavum nallōrē irakkam mikutiyāl annōy koṇṭāratu kaṉaviṟṟōṉṟi atu tīrkkum marunti aṟivittuc celkiṉṟaṉarō, aṉṟi nōyāḷikku uṟaviṉarāy uḷḷār avar paṭuntuyari niṉintu ataṉit tīrttaṟkuriya maruntiṉi ārāyum nallaṟivē an nōyāḷiyiṉ kaṉaviṉkaṭ ṭōṉṟi ataṉi yoḻikkum vaḻi kāṭṭukiṉṟatō, am mūṉṟil itu tāṉ uṟutiyeṉṟu tīrmāṉittal iyalātatā yirukkiṉṟatu. eṉṟālum ittakiyatōr aṟivippu nōyāḷiyiṉ kaṉaviṉkaṭ ṭōṉṟutalum, avvaṟivippiṉ paṭiyē ceyya avarkku annōy nīṅkutalum maṟukka ēlāta uṇmi nikaḻccikaḷāy āṅkāṅkuk kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. uṭalnalat  tūtuvaṉ eṉṉum veḷiyīṭṭiṉ āciriyarum pukaḻpeṟṟa amerikka maruttuvarumāṉa hālpurūk eṉpavar tamakku vanta koṭiyatōr irumal nōy tīrnta varalāṟṟiṉip piṉvarumāṟu eḻuti aṟivittirukkiṉṟār:
1870 ām āṇṭu iḷavēṉiṟ kālattil yāṉ mikak koṭiyatōr irumalnōyāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭēṉ. ovvoru kārkālattum iḷavēṉiṟkālattum annōy eṉṉit toṭarntu paṟṟip pallāṇṭukaḷ tuṉpuṟuttivantamiyāl atu nāṭpaṭṭa nōyāy vērūṉṟi viṭumō veṉavum atuvē eṉatuyirkku iṟutiyāy muṭiyumō veṉavum peritum uḷaṅ kalaṅkiṉēṉ. appōtu yāṉ iḷiñaṉāy iruntamiyālum, nīṇṭa kālaṅ kikkoṇṭu ōḻukattakkatoru toḻili appoḻututāṉ yāṉ mēṟkoṇṭamiyālum, eṉatu varuṅkāla niliyiṉit terintu mikavuñ cōrvaṭintēṉ. ivvāṟu cōrvukoṇṭa niliyil orukāl uṟakkaṅ
koṇṭēṉ; āṉālum, aḵtu āḻnta tuyil aṉṟu; appōtu, piṟ collappaṭum makiḻcciyāṉatu iṉṉum eṉ maṉattiviṭṭuakalāta paṭiyāyt tōṉṟalāyiṟṟu. irupaḵtu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉāka iṟantupaṭṭamiyāl, ēṟakkuṟiya eṉatu niṉiviviṭṭu nīṅkippōṉa eṉ uṭaṉpiṟantāḷ, eṉatu paṭukkiyaṇṭi vantu niṉṟu, niṉ uṭal nalattippaṟṟi nī kavalippaṭātē; yām annōyi niṉakkut tīrkka vantirukkiṉṟōm; iṉṉum nī ulakattiṟ ceyyavēṇṭuvatu mikutiyāy uḷḷatu, eṉak kūṟiṉaḷ. avvāṟu kūṟiya piṉ avaḷ maṟintupōyiṉaḷ. oru miṉṉaṟ poṟi (Electric Battery) yiliruntu tōṉṟiya miṉṉāṟṟal eṉatu mūḷiyiṉūṭuruvi niṟintāleṉa eṉakku ōr uṇarcci tōṉṟiyatu; āṉālum, aḵtu eṉakkut tuṉpamāyillāmal iṉpamākavē kāṇap paṭṭatu. avvāṟu tiṭumeṉat tōṉṟiya ammiṉṉāṟṟal, mūḷiyiliruntu kīḻ iṟaṅkip paravalāyiṟṟu; eṉṉuṭiya neñcilum mūccuppiyilum atu mikka valivuṭaṉ nirampi niṟṟaluṇarntēṉ. piṉṉar aṅkiruntu atu kikāl muṉikaḷ variyiṟ paravavum. ammuṉikaḷil aḵtu iṉitāyc cuṭarntu eriyavuṅ kaṇṭēṉ. uṭaṉē yāṉ attuyil nīṅki viḻittēṉ; viḻittatum yāṉ muḻunalam peṟṟiruttal aṟintēṉ. atumutal annōy eṉakkut tirumpa vantatēyilli. eṉ uṭaṉ piṟantāḷiṉ uruvam attuṇit teḷivāka eṉakkut tōṉṟāviṭṭālum, avaḷatu kuraloli maṭṭum mikavun teḷivāyk kēṭṭatu. itaṟku muṉṉum piṉṉum ittakiya toru nikaḻcci eṉakkut tōṉṟiyatē yilli, eṉpatu.
iṉi, ittakiya nikaḻccikaḷ ematu vāḻnāḷiṟ cila muṟi empāl nērntaṉavēṉum, avaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭu iṉṉum ematu uḷatti viṭṭakalāmal mikavun teḷivākap patintiruttalāl, aviyiṟṟil oṉṟi iṅkē eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉam:
yām eḻutum nūl ovvoṉṟum paṉṉāḷum pala muṟiyum āyntāyntu eḻutappaṭuvatu. yām eḻutuvaṉa ematuḷattiṟku iṉimitarum variyil, emataṟiviṟkup poruttamākak kāṇappaṭumvariyil avaṟṟip paṉmuṟiyum niṉintu niṉintu variyāmal melleṉa eḻutalē emakku vērūṉṟiya iyaṟkiyāy viṭṭatu. ataṉāl, yām eḻutum nūlkaḷi yāmē ematu celaviṟ patippiṭṭu veḷiyiṭuva taṉṟip, piṟar avvāṟu ceytaṟku avaṟṟik koṭuppa tilli. eṉṟālum, emmi irukāṟ koḻumpumā nakarkku varuvittu ematu tamiḻt toṇṭiṟku utavipala āṟṟiya aṉpar cilaruḷ oruvariṉ akkāla maṉaniliyiṉi viyantu,  avarkkuk kimmāṟoṉṟu ceyya viḻintu, yām eḻutiya tiruvācaka virivuriyiṉi avar veḷiyiṭak koṭuttēm. avar ataṟkāka emakkup poruḷutaviyuñ ceytu vantāreṉiṉum, yām tiruvācakac ceyyuṭkaḷi āḻntārāyn teḻutik koṭuttu varukiyil, uriyiṉi virinteḻutit tarumāṟu iṭi yiṭiyē emmi avar vaṉcoṟkaḷāl nerukki vantaṉar. iravil oṉpatu maṇikkumēṟ kaṇviḻittiruntu ētum eḻututal ceyyāta yām, avartam vaṉcoṟkaḷip poṟukka ēlāmal, toṭarpākac cilanāṭ patiṉorumaṇi variyiṟ kaṇviḻittiruntu ataṉi eḻuti varalāyiṉēm. avvāṟu ceytuvaravē, ematu mūḷiyiṟ cūṭēṟi mayakkam mikuntatu; uṇṭa ciṟituṇavuñ ceriyāmaṟ kakkalāyiṟṟu; uṭampeṅkuṅ kāyccalum uṇṭāyiṟṟu; iruppil iruntāl tāṅka muṭiyāta maṇṭik kiṟukiṟuppu vantatu; ataṉāṟ paṭukkiyilēyē kiṭakkalāyiṉēm; iṅṅaṉam tuṉpuṟṟuk koṇṭu vanta cila nāṭkaḷilellām ematu kīḻvayiṟṟiṉ valappakkattiṉuḷḷē oru kuṭaku nārattam paḻa aḷaviṉatāṉa oru kaḻalikkaṭṭi yuṇṭāyiṟṟu. ak kaṭṭiyuṇṭāṉapiṉ, ematuṭampil viṭākkāyccal vīciyatu; paṭukkiyiṟ piṟarutaviyiṉṟi appuṟam ippuṟam puraḷvatum eḻuntiruppatum iyalāvāyiṉa. varavara uṭampum melintatu. vantanōy mikuntu varutalaṉṟic ciṟitum taṇintilāmiyiṉ, emakku an nōy tīrkka aṉpiṉāl vantutaviya valla maruttuvarkaḷ uḷaṅkalaṅkiṉar. emakkum uyirvāḻkkiyil nampikki yaṟṟatu eṉṟālum, ematuḷattiṟ kalakkammaṭṭum uṇṭāka villi. aḵtu ētukāraṇattālō ippōtu ataṉi aṟital iyalavilli. ittuṇiyaṟṟa nilimiyil, emakku inta nila ulaka vāḻvu iṉṟu oḻiyumō! nāḷi yoḻiyumō! eṉṟu etirpārttirukkuṅ kālattil, oru nāḷ iravu viṭivataṟku iraṇṭu maṇinērattiṟku muṉṉē emakku oru tōṟṟam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. atu kaṉavu niliyō, naṉavu niliyō iṉṉateṉṟu tuṇintu collal kūṭavilli. emmiccūḻa oru pēriruḷ tōṉṟiyatu. avviruḷil evarō cilar ulavum aravanteṉpaṭṭatu. avviruḷiyum ataṉkaṇ ulavuvāratu aravattiyuṅ kaṇṭu kēṭṭa ematuḷḷam peritu naṭuṅkiyatu. annaṭukkam uṇṭāṉa ciṟitu nērattilellām, ap pēr iruḷiṉkaṇ emmic cūḻap paḷapaḷappāṉa or oḷi tōṉṟiyatu. uṭaṉē, pārppataṟku iṉiya muṉivariyotta mika veṇmiyāṉa ōr āṅkilat turimakaṉāratu tiruvuruvam ematarukē kiyil oru kaṇṇāṭi viḷakkip piṭittuk koṇṭu vantutu, at turi makaṉāratu tiruvuruvattik kāṇṭalum ematuḷattē yirunta naṭukkam muṟṟum oḻintupōka, yām muṉṉaṟiyāta oru makiḻcciyum iṉpa uṇarvum varappeṟṟēm. avaratu uruvattiṉuyarañ ciṟitu kuḷḷamā yiruntatu; avaratu yākki tiṇṇiyatu; talimēṟ kuṟukiya mayirum mōvāyil nīṇṭa tāṭi mayirum narittu veṇmiyākat tōṉṟiṉa; ciṟitēṟakkuṟiya eḻupattintu allatu eṇpatu āṇṭiṟku mēṟpaṭṭavarākak kāṇappaṭṭaṉar; mika veṇmiyāṉa niṟamuṭiyarāy āṅkila makkaḷip pōlavē mēluṅ kīḻum veḷḷiya caṭṭi yaṇi tiruntaṉar; iruntavāṟṟāl, tiruvācakatti āṅkilattil moḻipeyartta pōpputturi yavarkaḷiṉ vaṭivattiyē avar ottiruntaṉar. iṅṅaṉan tōṉṟiya at turimakaṉār tamatu kiyiṟ piṭittirunta kaṇṇāṭi viḷakkuṭaṉ emmic cuṟṟiccuṟṟi naṭantu, emakkuc ciṟitu toliviṟ cūḻattōṉṟiya ap pēr iruḷil iyaṅkiṉa cilarik kaṭintu pēciṉār; ivarāl ivvulakiṟku evvaḷavō naṉmi viḷiyavēṇṭi yirukkiṉṟatu. Mjyhš, Ú§fŸ ïtiu mQfhkš Xo¥ ngh§fŸ! eṉṟutamiḻiṟpēci avarkaḷit turattiṉār. avvaḷavil yām tiṭumeṉa viḻittuk koṇṭēm. viḻitta ciṟitu nērattilellām poḻutu viṭintatu. ematuṭampiṟ kāyccalum illi; aṭivayiṟṟiṉ valatupuṟat tiruntakaḻalik kaṭṭiyiyuṅ kāṇām. aṉṟu emmi vantu kaṇṭa maruttuvarum piṟarumellām attuṇi virivil akkoṭu nōy nīṅkiyatu kaṇṭu mikkatōr iṟumpū teytiṉar.
avviravil empāl aṅṅaṉam vantu nōy tīrtta appuṉita uruvam namatu arumit tiruvācakat tamiḻ māmaṟiyil aḷaviṟanta aṉpuvittirunta pōpput turiyavarkaḷiṉ āvi vaṭivāyirukkalāmō, allatu mēlulakattil vikum atturimakaṉāriṉ nalleṇṇamē avaraturuvattiṉit tōṟṟuvittu emakku vanta ap pēriṭari nīkkiyatō iṉṉatu tāṉ eṉṟurittal iyalātu. yām appōtu tiruvācaka virivuriyeḻuti vantamiyāl, ataṟku iṭiyūṟu nēralākāteṉṟu niṉintē, tiruvācakatti yeṇṇi yeṇṇi yurukiya pōpput turiyavarkaḷē aṅṅaṉam vantu emakku nōy tīrttārākal vēṇṭu meṉpatē ematu muṭipu. vanta avvāṅkilat turimakaṉār tamakkuriya āṅkila moḻiyiṟ pēcātu, centamiḻiṟ pēci, emmic cūḻnta iruḷil ulāya tīya āvikaḷit turattiyatum yāṅkoṇṭa muṭipiṉiyē valuppaṭuttukiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉamē piṉṉarorukālum yāṅ kaṭuṅkāyccal nōyāl tuṉpuṟṟa vaḻip paruttuyarnta karumēṉiyum maḻitta taliyum aruṭpārviyum uṭiya oru tuṟaviyiṉ uruvam ematu kaṉaviṉkaṭṭōṉṟi emakku annōy tīrttaruḷiyatu.
ittakiya kaṉā nikaḻccikaḷ ematu vāḻnāḷil mika ariyavāyc cila muṟikaḷē tōṉṟiṉa. ēṉikkaṉā nikaḻccikaḷ perumpālum ematu niṉivukku varuvatilli. mēleṭuttuk kāṭṭiyavipōṉṟa ciṟcilavē ippōtu em maṉakkaṇṇetirē tōṉṟā niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṉum, pirāliṉ ākaṭu mūlar eṉap peyariya oru mātarār tamatu niṉiviṉ orumiyāl vēṟorumātarkkuk kaṉaviṉkaṭ ceṉṟu nōytīrtta varalāṟṟiṉiyum iṅkē eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvām: immātarār iruntāṟ pōliruntu tiṭumeṉa aṟituyiliṟ cellum iyaṟki vāyntavar. ivar aṅṅaṉam aṟituyiliṟ ceṉṟirukkuṅkāl tammuṭam piluḷḷa nōykaḷiyum piṟaruṭampiluḷḷa nōykaḷiyun teḷivāy eṭuttuk kūṟiyatōṭu annōy tīrkkum eḷiya iyaṟki muṟikaḷiyum naṉku viḷakkic colvar. avi maṭṭumē yaṉṟip, piṟaruḷḷattil uḷḷa eṇṇaṅkaḷiyum avar tam naṭakkikaḷiyuṅkūṭa veḷippaṭac colvar. tolivil viyaṉṉā nakarattiluḷḷa tam uṭaṉ piṟantāṉukkut tama turuvattit tām tōṟṟuvittal kūṭu meṉṟum urippar. tammuṭiya kaṇkaḷi iṟukak kaṭṭiviṭṭālum avar nāṭakacālic cīṭṭukaḷiyum icinūliluḷḷa pāṭṭu
kaḷiyun kaṇṇāṟ pārttup paṭippatupōṟ paṭittuc colvar. ivarkku nēyarāṉa oru maruttuva āciriyar orukāl immātari nōkkit, tamatu kuṭumpattiṟ cila nāṭkaḷukku muṉ nikaḻntatu yāteṉṟu viṉava, uṭaṉē avvammiyār, patiṉintu kaltoliviluḷḷa oru paṭṭiṉattil uṅkaḷ māmaṉār iṟantupaṭṭār eṉa nuvaṉṟār; aḵtuṇmi yākavē iruntatu.
ittakiya ākaṭu mātarārkku naṇparāṉa maṟṟoru maṅkiyār tām appōtu palvaliyāṟ peritum varuntik koṇṭiruntatāl, aṭutta nāḷ vaḻakkam pōl avari vantu pārttal iyalāteṉak kūṟiṉār; appaṭiyāyiṉ yāṉ iṉṟiravu nuṅkaḷivantu kāṇpēṉ eṉap pukaṉṟār. pukaṉṟapaṭiyē, tam naṇparāṉa maṅkiyār palvaliyāl tuṉpuṟṟup paṭuttirukkum avvaṟikkuḷ ammātariṉ uruvam irāppoḻutiṟkuriya uṭiyaṇinta vaṇṇamāyt tōṉṟi, ammaṅki yāruṭaṉ pakkattiṟ paṭuttukkiṭantatu. kāliyiṟ kaṇviḻitta ammaṅkiyār tamakkirunta palvali muṟṟumē nīṅkippōkakkaṇṭu peritum viyappaṭintār. ākaṭu ammiyār tamatu illattiṉkaṇ tamatu paṭukkiyi viṭṭu akalātiruntum, avar eṅṅaṉan tamatu illattiṟpukuntu tammuṭaṉ paṭuttiruntār? eṉpati niṉintu ammaṅki yār aṭinta viyappiṉukku ōr aḷavē yilli.
iṉi, naṉavuṇarviṉāl evvāṟṟāṉum aṟintu koḷḷal iyalāta kalvip poruḷkaḷ cila kaṉaviṉ kaṇ iṉituviḷaṅkit tōṉṟiya varalāṟukaḷum pala; avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟi iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭutum:
pāpilōṉiya tēyattil nippūr eṉṉum nakarattiṉ kaṭ pallāyira āṇṭukaṭku muṉ aḻintupaṭṭa araṇmaṉikaḷilē paṇṭināḷ aracarkaḷāṟ cetukki vikkappaṭṭa kalveṭṭukaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittu, avaṟṟiṟ collappaṭṭa kuṟippukaḷik koṇṭu, aññāṉṟi aracarkaḷ avar kīḻ uyir vāḻnta makkaḷuṭiya nākarika varalāṟukaḷi yellām ārāynteḻututaṟ poruṭṭu, annakarkkuc ceṉṟa jermaṉ āciriyarāṉa hilpirac (Professor Hilpecht) eṉpār āṇṭeṭutta iraṇṭu ciṟu mōtirat tuṇṭukaḷiṟ cetukkappaṭṭirunta coṟṟoṭarkaḷik kaṇṭaṟitaṟku evvaḷavō muyaṉṟum, ammuyaṟci avarkkuc ciṟitum payaṉ tantilatu. ēṉeṉṟāṟ, kaṇṭeṭutta ac ciṟutuṇṭukaḷ iraṇṭum orē mōtirattiṉ tuṇṭukaḷākak kāṇappaṭavilli. ākiyāl, ivaṟṟi evvaḷavutāṉ iṇittup pārttālum, avaṟṟiṉkaṭ cetukkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷ iṇintu oruporuḷ tantila. nippūrilirunta pakalavaṉ kōyil aḻintu kiṭakkum iṭatti akaḻntu tēṭiyatil, avviraṇṭu ciṟumōtirattuṇṭukaḷi yottavi piṉṉum paṉṉiraṇṭu kiṭittaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ mēlellāñ cetukkap
paṭṭa eḻuttukaḷ āppuvaṭivam vāyntiruntaṉa. avi cetukkappaṭṭa kālam, iṟṟikku 3000 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉṉē tuvaṅki 3600 āṇṭukaḷ variyilākum. ittuṇṭukaḷiyellām paṉṉāḷum paṉ muṟiyum avar iṇit tiṇittup pārttum, avaṟṟiṉ poruḷik kaṇṭaṟitaṟku evvaḷavō pāṭupaṭṭum, atu ciṟituṅ kikūṭavilli. piṉṉar orunāḷ naḷḷiravu maṭṭum avar ivvuḻippiṟ karutti ōyāmaṟ celuttip pārttum payaṉ ētum uṇṭākāmiyāl mikavum ayarvaṭintavarāyp paṭukkiyiṟ ceṉṟu āḻntu tuyilkoḷḷalāṉār. aṅṅaṉam avar āḻntu tuyilkiyil, anta nippūrp pakalavaṉ kōṭṭattiṟ paṇṭikkālattiṟ kurukkaḷāyirunta oruvariṉ uruvam avaratu akakkaṇṇetirē tōṉṟiyatu. avvuruvam nīṇṭu olliyāy, nāṟpatāṇṭu akaviyiṉatāyp, potuvāṉa uṭiyaṇintiruntatu. ivvāṟu tōṉṟiya avvuruvam, akkōyiliṉ teṉkiḻakkil uḷḷa tākiya kaḷañciya aṟikku avvāciriyari aḻittuc celvatākak kāṇappaṭṭatu. aṅkē ceṉṟatun, tāḻnta maccuṭiyatāyc cāḷaram ētum illātatāyc ciṟiyatāy ataṉuḷḷirunta maṟṟōraṟikkuḷ avvāciriyari atu kūṭṭicceṉṟatu; ac ciṟṟaṟikkuḷ oru periya marappeṭṭiyiruntatu; nilattilē oruvaki maṇikkal (agate) liṉ tuṇṭukaḷum viṭūriyaṅkaḷum iṟintu kiṭantaṉa. avvaṟik kuṭ ceṉṟatum avvuruvam avvāciriyari nōkki, 22, 26 ām pakkaṅkaḷil nīr taṉittaṉiyē veḷippaṭutti yirukkiṉṟa iraṇṭu tuṇṭukaḷum viralil aṇiyum mōtiraṅkaḷ alla, avaṟṟiṉ varalāṟu itu: - ki.mu. 1300 il irunta aracaṉ kuṟikaḻuveṉpāṉ, orukāṟ pakalavaṉ kōyilukkut tāṉ cērppitta ēṉi maṇikkaṟkaḷ viṭūriyaṅkaḷuṭaṉ, eḻuttu veṭṭiya oru maṇik kaṟkuḻāyiṉiyum nērttikkaṭaṉākac celuttiyiruntāṉ. ataṉpiṟ caṭutiyilē, niṉippu eṉṉuṅ kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvuruvattiṟkāka maṇikkalliṟ camitta oru cōṭu kātaṇikaḷ ceyyumpaṭi aracaṉiṭamiruntu kurukkaḷākiya eṅkaḷukku oru kaṭṭaḷi vantatu. anta nērattil eṅkaḷ kiyil maṇikkal ētum illāmiyāl yāṅkaḷ peruntikil koṇṭōm. aracaṉiṭṭa kaṭṭaḷip paṭi ceytaṟku avaṉ celuttivitta ammaṇikkaṟkuḻāyi mūṉṟu kūṟāka aṟuttu, mūṉṟu vaḷiyaṅkaḷ ceyvatit tavira eṅkaḷukkuvēṟu vaḻiyilli. akkuḻāyiṉmēṟ cetukkappaṭṭirunta eḻuttuttoṭar ataṉāl mūṉṟu kūṟāka aṟuntu ovvoru kūṟum ovvoru vaḷiyattiṉ kaṇ iruppatāyiṟṟu. mutaliraṇṭu vaḷiyaṅkaḷum akkaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvuruvattiṟkuk kātaṇi kaḷāyiṉa; numakku attaṉi varuttatti viḷivitta attuṇṭukaḷiraṇṭum akkātaṇi kaḷiṉ pakutikaḷākum, avviraṇṭiyum oṉṟu cērttup pārppīrāṉāl eṉ colliṉuṟuti pulaṉākum. āṉāl, mūṉṟām vaḷiyatti nīr itukāṟum akaḻntu pārttuṅ kaṇṭeṭukkavilli. aḵtu orukālum numakku akappaṭātu. eṉṟu kūṟiya aḷavil maṟintupōyatu. uṭaṉē avvāciriyar viḻitteḻuntu tāṅ kaṇṭa akkaṉāviṉit tam maṉiviyārkku eṭutturittār. aṭutta nāḷ ñāyiṟṟukkiḻami kāliyil avviraṇṭu tuṇṭukaḷiyum, tamakkuk kaṉaviṟ ṟōṉṟiya kurukkaḷ uruvam moḻinta vaṇṇamē oṉṟu cērttup pārkka, avi peritum viyakkat takkavakiyāy oruṅku poruntip, pakalavaṉ putalvarāṉa niṉippuk kaṭavuḷākiya taṉ perumāṉukkum pakalavaṉiṉ peruṅkurukkaḷāṉa kuṟikaḻuveṉpāṉ kāṇikkiyāka aḷittatu itu veṉap poruḷ payantatu.
 pārmiṉkaḷ! iṟṟikku 3200 āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ aḻintupaṭṭa nippūrp pakalavaṉ kōṭṭattiliruntu akaḻnteṭutta vaḷiyat tuṇṭukaḷiṟ cetukkappaṭṭa coṟṟoṭar, naṉaviṉkaṇ evvaḷavō ārāyntu pārttum oru ciṟitum pulappaṭātupōka, maṟṟu atu kaṉaviṉkaṭṭōṉṟi aṟivittu kurukkaḷiṉ vāymoḻiyāl naṉku pulaṉāyiṟṟu. mika ariya ivvuṇmi nikaḻcciyiṉi nuṇuki ārāynta airōppiya āciriyarkaḷ, attuṇṭukaḷi akaḻnteṭutta hilpiracceṉpār nāṇāḷum naṉaviṉkaṇ ārāyntu koṇṭu iṭarppaṭṭa varātalālum, akkōyiṟ ciṟṟaṟiyiṉ varalāṟṟiṉi maṟṟōrāciriyarpāṟ kēṭṭiruntu ataṉi maṟantupōyiṉum avaratu avvārāycci yaṟivu kaṉavuṭaṉpiṉ kaṭṭeḷivu mika vuṭiyatāy ataṉi niṉivukūrntu iyaṅkiṉamiyālum, avaratu nuṇṇaṟivē kaṉaviṉkaṇ accoṟṟoṭari iṇikkum vaḻikāṭṭi, ataṟku ak kōyiṟ kuruviṉuruvattiyun tōṟṟuvittateṉak kūṟā niṟkiṉṟaṉar. avar kūṟum avviḷakkam evvaḷavil ēṟṟukkoḷḷaṟ pālateṉpatu aṟiñarkaḷē muṭivukaṭṭaṟ pālār. mūvāyirattu irunūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ appakalavaṉ kōṭṭattilirunta oru kurukkaḷiṉ uruvattiyum, avvuruvam eṭutturitta uṇmikaḷiyum ellām, naṉaviṉkaṇ iṭarppaṭṭa hilpirac āciriyaratu nuṇṇuṇarvē kaṉaviṉkaṭ kaṟpittuviṭṭaṉa eṉṉum uri ematuḷḷattiṟkup poruttamuṭiyatākak kāṇappaṭavilli. emataṟivukkuṭ paṭṭa maṭṭiṟ, paṇṭirunta akkurukkaḷiṉ uruvamum atu moḻinta uṇmikaḷum avvāciriyaratu niṉivē kaṟpittaṉa ākā. uyarntōr. ceyyum ariya aṟivumuyaṟcikaḷ payaṉpaṭāvāyppōka, avar paṭum pāṭṭiṉit tolivil nuṇṇuṭampiliruntu aṟiyum āṉṟōriṉ āviyō, allatu avaratu niṉivō, aṅṅaṉam iṭarppaṭṭu varuntum aṟiñarkkuk kaṉaviṉkaṭ ṭōṉṟi uṇmiyi aṟivittu avartam iṭarppāṭṭiṉi nīkkic collaniṟkiṉṟa teṉpatē emakkup poruttamuṭiyatākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. atu niṟka.
iṉṉuñ, camayapporuḷ virivuriceyvatil mikac ciṟantavarāṉa camayakuru oruvar (spurgeoj) orukāl, oru ciṟappu nāḷiṟ ceyyavēṇṭiya virivuriyoṉṟaṉi eḻutuvataṟkākat tamatu karuttiṉi orumukappaṭutti amarntu ataṉic ceyyap pukuntār. āṉāl, aṉṟikku avarāl aḵtu aḻakitāka eḻuti muṭikkappaṭutaṟku iṭantarāmaṟ pēriṭar payappatāyiṟṟu, evvaḷavō muyaṉṟum, aḵtu ōr uruppaṭāmiyāl maṉattaḷarcci yuṭiyarāy atu ceyvati viṭṭeḻuntār; eṉṟālum avvirivuri yaṭutta nāḷiṟ ceyyappaṭa vēṇṭuvatātalāl, atupaṟṟi avar peritum maṉaṅkavaṉṟu ayarcciyāṟ paṭukkiyiṟkiṭantu uṟaṅkiṉār. aṅṅaṉam uṟaṅkukiyil avar naḷḷiraviṟ ṟiṭumeṉa eḻuntu niṉṟu avvirivuriyiṉi mikka kiḷarcciyuṭaṉum urukkattuṭaṉum oru toṭarpāka oḻuṅkākap pēcuvārāṉār. avar aṅṅaṉam virivuri nikaḻttum aravaṅ kēṭṭut tuyiloḻinta avartam maṉiviyār ataṉi arukiruntu kēṭṭup peruviyappaṭintār. avvāṟu avar virivuri nikaḻttiyapiṉ tirumpavum paṭukkiyil amarntu uṟaṅkiviṭṭār. maṟunāṭ kāliyil, avartam maṉiviyār avarkku annaḷḷiravu nikaḻcciyiṉit terivitta piṟakutāṉ, avar avvirivuri tamatu kaṉaviṉkaṇ ōr uruvāki oḻuṅkupaṭṭati niṉivu kūrntār.
mēṟpōnta uṇmi nikaḻccikaḷākiya meyccāṉṟukaḷ koṇṭu, kalviyārāycciyiṟ karuttūṉṟi niṟpārkku avartam ārāyccit tuṟiyil nērum iṭarppāṭukaḷi, nuṇṇuṭampiṟ ceṉṟa avaratu nuṇṇuṇavarvō, aṉṟi mēlulakaṅkaḷil uṟivārāṉa kalivallāriṉ āvikaḷō, aṉṟi akkalivallāriṉ niṉivukaḷō avartaṅ kaṉaviṟ ṟōṉṟi nīkki, avar tamakku meyyaṟivuccuṭar koḷuvum uṇmi yiṉit teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka.
iṉip, piṉ nikaḻappōkum iṭarkaḷ kaṉaviṉ kaṇ muṉ aṟivikkappaṭutalum āṅkāṅku naṭipeṟāniṟkiṉṟatu. itaṟku meyyāka nikaḻnta cilavaṟṟi iṅkeṭuttuk kāṭṭutum.
1890 ām āṇṭu cūlittiṅkaḷ 12 ām nāḷ, oru turimakaṉār (William Tudor) tamakku vēṇṭiya nilaṅkaḷi vāṅkutaṟkeṉṟu, tām irunta ūriviṭṭu veḷiyūr oṉṟiṟ ceṉṟiruntavar, aṉṟiravu paṭukkikkuc ceṉṟār. ceṉṟu naṉṟāka ayarntuṟaṅkiṉavar, naḷḷiravil tam maṉiviyār tamatu peyariccolli aḻikkakkēṭṭu viḻitteḻuntu paṭukkimēl uṭkārntār. uṭkārntu tām ōr ūrilun tam maṉiviyār maṟṟōr ūrilum iruttali niṉivukūrntu, tirumpavum viḻuntu paṭuttuk koṇṭār. eṉṟālum, maṟunāṭ kāliyil eḻuntatu mutal, avar muṉṉāḷ iravil tam maṉiviyār tammip peyarcolli aḻitta oliyiṉi niṉintu peritum maṉaṅkalaṅkuvārāṉār. nāṭōṟun tam maṉiviyāriṭamiruntu kaṭitam varum vaḻakkam pōṟ cilanāṭkaḷākak kaṭitam varāmiyiṉiyum niṉikkavē avarkku ak kalakkam piṉṉum mikuvatāyiṟṟu. ākavē, avar tam maṉiviyārkku uṭaṉē oru kampicceytiviṭuttār. ataṟku ētoru viṭiyum varāmiyāl, maṟupaṭiyum oru kampic ceyti viṭuttār. ataṟkum viṭi vantilāmiyāl, tam maṉivikkuttāṉ uṭampu nalamilliyō, allatu tampiḷḷikaḷuḷ evarēṉuntām nōyvāyppaṭṭaṉarō, allatu tam neruṅkiya uṟaviṉarevarkkēṉun tān tīṅku nērntatō, allatu avaril evarēṉuntām iṟantu paṭṭaṉarō eṉṟu palavāṟṟā ṉellām eṇṇiyeṇṇi avar tuṉpuṟalāṉār. piṟaku iraṇṭoru nāḷil avar tam maṉiviyāriṭamiruntu oru kaṭitam vantatu. vanta ak kaṭitam, atturimakaṉāriṉ marumakaṉ cūli 11 ām nāḷ naḷḷiravil oru miṉvaṇṭiyiṉ etirē viḻuntu paṭukāyam aṭintaṉa ṉeṉṟum, oṟṟikkāl pōyviṭṭatēṉum avaṉ uyirpiḻittukkoṇṭā ṉeṉṟum aṟivippa tāyiṟṟu. avar tam maṉiviyār attuyaranikaḻcciyip piṉṉāṭ piṟpakal aṟintu, aṉṟiravellām peruntuyar kūrntu taṅkaṇavariyum niṉintiruntārātalāl avaratu anniṉivē tolivil vēṟōr ūrilirunta avartaṅ kaṇavari atē nāḷ iravil aḻippatāyiṟṟu eṉka.
iṉṉuṅ, kolampā eṉṉum muṉivar orunāḷiravutan tiruk kūṭṭattuṭaṉ tuyilkoṇṭiruppuḻic caṭutiyil tuyil oḻintu tan tirukkūṭṭattavari yeḻuppit, taṟiyiṟ kaṭṭiyirukkum paṭaki yaviḻttut tām irukkum iṭattiṟku arukāmiyil ataṉik koṇarntu niṟuttukaveṉa avarkku moḻintār. ēṉeṉṟāl, avarkaḷ ap paṭaki muṉṉē niṟuttiyirunta karippakkattiluḷḷa ciṟṟūr neruppukku iriyākappōtali avar eṅṅaṉamākavō muṉṉarē terintu koṇṭār. avar kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭa vaṇṇamē ap paṭaku avarkaṭku aṇmiyiṟ koṇarntu niṟuttappaṭṭa ciṟitu nērattilellām, avar kūṟiya ac ciṟṟūr neruppuppaṟṟi yeritali avarkaḷ ellāruṅ kaṇṭu viyappuṟṟaṉar.
aṭikkuṟippukaḷ
1.	Captin Scott.
2.	T.V. Ivey to Prof. William James
3.	“Seeing the Invisible” 1917, pp.243, 244
14. naṉaviṟṟōṉṟum muṉṉaṟivippukaḷ
iṉṉum,ivvintiya nāṭṭiṉ araciyaṟpaṭikkut talivarāyirunta rāparṭ eṉpār orukāṟ kāyūl nakari viṭṭuk kipar kaṇavāyvariyil mikuvirivāyk kutiriyūrntu varukiyil, tiṭumeṉa avaruḷḷattil ōr uṇarvu tōṉṟip piṉvarum pēriṭari aṟivittu, avar ataṟkumēl cellāmal avarit taṭiceytatu, avar ataṉāl aṅṅaṉan taṭippaṭṭu niṉṟa ciṟitu nērattilellān, tamakkukkīḻt talivarāyuḷḷār iruvar avarpāṟ kaṭukip pōntu, kāntārattiluḷḷa tammavar paṭiyāṉatu pakivar talivaṉāl muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭu muṟṟumi ceyyap
paṭṭirukkum mika viyappāṉa ceytiyit terivittār. atukēṭṭatum, avar kāntārattiṟkut tirumpicceṉṟu, tamatu paṭikku vanta ap pēriṭari nīkkip peruñciṟappup peṟṟār.
